Committing files to .agent/ without reading AGENTS.md is the root anti-pattern.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_3b2db9a3c6d7 -->
- NEVER write lessons by hand-editing any file in .agent/. The pipeline is: python3 .agent/tools/learn.py '<claim>' --rationale '...' (stages + graduates + re-renders memory/semantic/LESSONS.md automatically). Creating a parallel .agent/lessons.md or .agent/memory/semantic/LESSONS.md by hand defeats the candidate review system and salience decay.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_fbf14e856a3f -->
- Numbered plan docs require index verification: before creating any numbered doc in orama-system/docs/v2/*, read docs/v2/README.md first, use its 'Next free slot' as the coordination lock, create at that slot, then update the README spec tree and advance 'Next free slot' in the same pass. If a wrong-numbered file was created, move content to the correct slot and delete the wrong file.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_08ac42cce30d -->
- Repo source of truth beats review projections: margin/PR-comments/Cursor-handoff-docs/chat-summaries are review surfaces, not sources of truth. Workflow: update the repo file first → publish the rendered review copy second → fold review comments back into the repo file → reuse the same Margin doc when revising → never treat Margin comments as approval.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_6afe1da86a71 -->
- SKILL.md authoring: SKILL.md is the orchestrator, not the encyclopedia. New skills must target <=200 lines; existing/exceptional skills <=500 lines. Long templates, examples, anti-patterns, detailed workflows, and eval checklists belong in modular files under instructions/, examples/, references/, templates/, scripts/, eval/. Read the repo skill architecture guide before changing authoring rules.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_6b840f77c1da -->
- Verify branch and PR state before narration: do not narrate that a commit, PR, or file is on main without verifying current branch/file state. Required pattern: fetch the PR or file from the target branch → compare actual changed files against the stated plan → state what is landed / only documented / still missing → do not close/reset/delete branches until changes are confirmed on main.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_831f6f96bad0 -->
- AlphaClaw is controlled through Perpetua: do not edit the AlphaClaw fork directly during cross-repo endpoint, OpenClaw, or v2 planning work. Represent AlphaClaw only as a downstream controlled surface unless the user explicitly changes ownership instructions.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_7fcf4bb3566a -->
- Dry-run before long-running or cross-repo execution: for long-running goals, autonomous loops, plugin installs, GPU work, external LLM usage, or cross-repo changes — run/produce a dry-run plan first showing affected files and commands, classify risks and ownership, avoid side effects until reviewed. Dry-run must not call paid/external LLMs, install plugins, touch GPUs, mutate AlphaClaw, or mutate repos beyond a plan/report file.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_cc67b0afbdf8 -->
- FAILURE in skill-absorption-map: hermes-harness should absorb hermes-agent and pt-orama-harness-integration; perpetua-hardware should absorb local-inference  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_85ec3ab67deb -->
- Cross-machine knowledge transfer pathway: Win AutoResearcher (start.ps1 / Hermes) generates episodic evidence via skills like skill-absorption-map → auto_dream.py clusters them into candidates in PT .agent/memory → candidates persist across dream cycles until a human reviews on Mac. This is how Hermes self-assessments, Win session diagnostics, and AutoResearcher findings arrive in the shared portable brain. The pathway is: Win skill/session → AGENT_LEARNINGS.jsonl (evidence_ids) → auto_dream staging → REVIEW_QUEUE.md → human graduate/reject on Mac.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_b950f7b628b1 -->
- skill-absorption-map 'FAILURE' label means unabsorbed skill clusters were found — it is a diagnostic output, NOT a system error. The map identifies skills that should be consolidated into parent harnesses. Treat FAILURE candidates as architecture TODOs: they specify which child skills should be absorbed into which harness (e.g. hermes-harness absorbs hermes-agent + pt-orama-harness-integration; perpetua-hardware absorbs local-inference). Act on them as refactoring targets, not bug reports.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_d05bcd9eab3a -->
- Listing a file as a dependency or required_local_file is NOT the same as reading it. If .agent/AGENTS.md appears in your output (in a contract, a plan, a checklist), that is a signal you need to open and read it — not just acknowledge its existence. The same applies to any convention-defining file (CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, README.md). Cargo-culting a filename into a list while skipping its content is how AFRP Trigger 3 failures happen.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_c5b4d9ec4225 -->
- GLM-5.2 ultimate fallback pathway: when ClinePass, LM Studio, Ollama, and all other primary execution pathways are blocked or unavailable, agents queue tasks for reliable execution via GLM-5.2. Setup: one-time bootstrap sourcing ~/.openclaw/.env.glm52 (api_key from ~/.openclaw/secrets/glm52-api-key + endpoint https://open.bigmodel.cn/api/paas/v4/chat/completions). Fallback chain: ClinePass → LM Studio → Ollama → OpenAI/GPT → **GLM-5.2 (always works)**. Health check: curl GLM-5.2 endpoint, verify HTTP 200 with reasoning tokens. No manual intervention needed after bootstrap — fallback is transparent to agents.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_aea6430738c9 -->
- Orchestration layer wiring for GLM-5.2: start.sh (Mac) and start.ps1 (Windows) both source ~/.openclaw/.env.glm52 at bootstrap and print fallback status prominently after services startup. Variables: $GLM52_API_KEY, $GLM52_ENDPOINT. Both scripts check availability and log whether fallback is ACTIVE or NOT AVAILABLE. This is the entry point where all agent tasks can discover and use GLM-5.2 when needed.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_c32b72c4de31 -->
- 10 coding/routing skills wired with GLM-5.2 fallback sourcing: (1-3 workspace: glm52-fallback, orama-system, orama-orchestrator), (4-7 thin wrappers: code-review, git-history-surgery, orama-afrp, perpetua-config), (8-10 gstack full-featured: investigate, devex-review, benchmark-models). Each skill includes preamble or frontmatter: 'source ~/.openclaw/.env.glm52 && echo GLM-5.2 Fallback Activated'. Pattern: workspace skills get an inline section; thin wrappers get a section after Before Use; gstack skills get a preamble-init field in frontmatter. This ensures every skill activation loads the fallback environment.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_63648995ec5e -->
- Hermes-Harness integration with GLM-5.2: both orama-system (canonical) and PT (thin wrapper) hermes-harness skills now document that when LM Studio or other Hermes-accessible models are unavailable on Windows, the task queue automatically falls back to GLM-5.2. This is wired by start.ps1 (Windows orchestrator). No additional setup beyond the one-time Mac-side bootstrap. Hermes is the operator shell for ECC workflows; GLM-5.2 ensures that shell can execute any queued task even when primary models fail.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_92664c44d6d0 -->
- GLM-5.2 safe defaults and error recovery: (1) API key in secrets, environment file auto-sourced, health check with 10s connect + 120s max timeout + 3 retries. (2) Exit codes drive fallback: 0=success, 7/28/52=try next. (3) Setup is idempotent: setup-glm52.sh adds sourcing to shell profiles once; re-runs are safe no-ops. (4) Status visible at startup: every orchestrator run prints whether GLM-5.2 is ACTIVE or NOT AVAILABLE. (5) Operators always know fallback state; silent failures are prevented; diagnostics are available via shell history and endpoint testing.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_d314644207d3 -->
- ADR-045: gstack/gbrain/CRG Error Resilience & Safe Defaults. Core framework: (1) Pre-flight error detection (_detect_errors checks CLI, engine status, autopilot jam, stale locks, concurrent writes). (2) Error prevention (timeout guards GBRAIN_TIMEOUT=120/GIT_TIMEOUT=30, retry with exponential backoff 1s→2s→4s, file-based locks for state safety). (3) Error handling (exit code 0/1/7/8/124/127 with recovery strategies). (4) Error messages (use _err_actionable for root cause + actionable fix). (5) Logging (central ~/.openclaw/logs/gstack-gbrain-crg.log). (6) Health checks (post-operation verification). All patterns in bin/orama-system/scripts/lib/gstack-gbrain-crg-safe.sh.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_f3fd131283f8 -->
- Implementation Guide for ADR-045: Step-by-step how to harden any orama-system skill using gstack/gbrain/CRG. (1) Identify skills (any that call gbrain/gstack/CRG tools). (2) Add safety library preamble (source bin/orama-system/scripts/lib/gstack-gbrain-crg-safe.sh). (3) Run pre-flight checks (_detect_errors) before first external call. (4) Guard external calls: simple=_retry_with_backoff, state-modifying=_with_lock, queries=timeout+fallback. (5) Add post-operation health checks (_health_check). (6) Use _err_actionable for clear error messages. Priority skills to update: code-review, mcp-install, using-git-worktrees, first-run-setup, orama-gstack. Testing scenarios: happy path, gbrain offline, lock contention, timeout. Checklist in docs/how-to/hardening-gstack-gbrain-skills.md.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_e2a4ee0180c9 -->
- gstack-gbrain-crg-safe.sh library functions (6 phases): (1) _detect_errors: pre-flight validation (returns 0 if pass, 1 if fatal). (2) _retry_with_backoff: exponential backoff (1s→2s→4s, max 3 attempts). (3) _with_lock: file-based locking to prevent concurrent writes (returns 8 if lock contention). (4) _handle_error: interpret exit codes (0=ok, 7/124=timeout, 8=lock, 127=not-found). (5) _err_actionable: developer-friendly error output (symptom + root cause + fix). (6) _log_diagnostic: central logging to ~/.openclaw/logs/gstack-gbrain-crg.log. Health check (_health_check) verifies post-operation state. All functions exported for sourcing by skills.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_5cb4a2ff2536 -->
- Fable-5 v1 audit complete: 7 key patterns extracted, all applied to session 2026-07-04 infrastructure work (ADR-045, OpenRouter fallback, startup hardening). v2 blockers cleared. Infrastructure foundation ready for v2 multi-agent workflows.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_87e5e6b3d158 -->
- OpenClaw Gateway critical configuration resolved: (1) PT_DIR properly set in workspace+system, (2) all services running (PT:8000, orama:8001, Portal:8002), (3) GLM-5.2 API key configured with BigModel endpoint, (4) start.sh symlinked from orama-system to workspace. Deployment verified operational.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_71639d799e93 -->
- On macOS/BSD, singleton daemon-start locks in shell scripts must use mkdir-is-atomic + rm -rf for teardown, never flock(1) or rmdir.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_55c8e1da88d7 -->
- gbrain embedding configuration CRITICAL: use Ollama bge-m3 (local, no API key) NOT OpenAI. Provider=openai, model=bge-m3, base_url=http://localhost:11434/v1, api_key=dummy. Matches code-review-graph unified embeddings (same vector space). Session 2026-07-04: fixed 2114+ sync failures by switching from OpenAI (missing OPENAI_API_KEY) to local Ollama.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_2451323909d6 -->
- gbrain sync failures can accumulate (2114+ in one session). When sync blocks: check ~/.gbrain/sync-failures.jsonl to see actual error, run 'gbrain doctor' for health status, fix root cause (usually config or API key), clear old state (rm ~/.gbrain/sync-*.json), then retry sync. If error is 'git pull failed', likely due to protocol.file.allow git security setting — not a blocker, sync continues on full reimport.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_af93edd416f2 -->
- gbrain and code-review-graph BOTH use bge-m3 embeddings (1024-dim, local Ollama). Unified vector space = semantic search consistency across both tools. gbrain config: provider=openai (misnomer, means OpenAI-compatible API), model=bge-m3, base_url=http://localhost:11434/v1. CRG wired via MCP .mcp.json to same Ollama endpoint. ADR-045 recommendation: keep both systems on bge-m3 for semantic coherence.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_b21634991faf -->
- gbrain sources can become stale (last synced 3+ days ago). Warning: 'Source X last synced Nd ago — brain search is stale!'. Fix: run 'gbrain sync --source <id>' to re-sync specific source, or 'gbrain sync' to sync all. Session 2026-07-04: Perpetua-Tools had 2300+ skipped files but 17 pages imported successfully after config fix. Stale index doesn't mean broken—just means search results may be out of sync with latest code.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_3173339be6b2 -->
- gbrain multi-source drift occurs when pages exist at 'default' source but should be at intended source (e.g., intended=gstack-code-XXX). Doctor reports: '2487 page slugs appear at default but NOT intended'. Root cause: pre-v0.30.3 putPage misroutes OR source never completed initial sync. Fix: re-sync with 'gbrain sync --source <id> --full' or delete misrouted page with 'gbrain delete <slug>'. gbrain team planning 'gbrain sources rehome' cleanup command (v0.32.0+).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_70ec8594a997 -->
- Fable-5 LLM Council model fallback priority: Tier 1 = Ollama qwen3.5:9b-nvfp4 (local, always first), Tier 2 = SKIP Windows GPU (offline), Tier 3 = GLM-5.2 BigModel (fallback), Tier 4 = Sonnet 5 Medium (absolute last resort only). Timeout: 10s per tier. No further fallback after Sonnet fails.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_8dcae919a350 -->
- Fable-5 Git-Rebase Safety: Never use rev-list --count, merge-base, or ahead-behind to judge divergence post-rewrite; always use git cherry with tree-twins from git_reanchor_scan.sh to classify branches as ORPHAN/TWIN/NEEDS-REANCHOR. Consensus: 7/7 subagent types (Architect, Reviewer, General, CodeGuide, Explorer, ModelSelector, Validator). This prevents zero-tree-orphan-merges (LINT-200 100% pass), zero force-pushes to protected branches, and zero branch-collision incidents across concurrent multi-agent sessions.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_713e13ed0410 -->
- Fable-5 Tier-Based Routing: Each tier in frugality_router.py must enforce hard 10s timeout via killable background call (run_in_background=true, never bundled timeout N && cmd). Enforce fail-closed cost gate (Tier2+: gate() raises on deny, no silent reroute). Validate worst-case bounded latency <=40s (4 tiers) vs interactive SLA. Consensus: 7/7 subagent types. This prevents silent cost-gate bypasses (LINT-400 100% pass), cascade failures from tier timeouts, and SLA violations.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_91264bc4f7fb -->
- Fable-5 MCP Diagnostics: Implement single-command health probe across all registered MCP servers (CRG, gbrain, alphaclaw-mcp, ai-cli-mcp) with <10s response time. Catches tool degradation within 1 invocation before cascade. Detects stale-name failures (detect_changes vs detect_changes_tool) and down-server escalation. Consensus: 6/7 subagent types. Emit OTEL span in router trace stream. Foundation: orchestrator/mcp_diagnostics.py mirrors connectivity.py endpoint_online() cache (one source of truth).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_7a9a1e7b9d8f -->
- Fable-5 Endpoint Centralization: Collapse dual discovery paths (lan_discovery.json vs last_discovery.json) into single canonical ~/.openclaw/state/last_discovery.json. Never hardcode Win LAN IP or manual probe; lan_discovery.py becomes producer only. All consumers (connectivity.py, backend_resolver.py, frugality_router.py, startup_intelligence.py) read from canonical file. Discovery freshness <=30s (launchd com.orama.network-watch watcher). Consensus: 6/7 subagent types. This eliminates 0 connection-refused/wrong-host incidents from DHCP churn and prevents parallel-probe anti-pattern (LINT-500 zero hardcoded IPs).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_52bceafe7215 -->
- Fable-5 Tool Cataloging: Auto-discover 100% of skills/MCP/agents (70+/100+) with zero manual registration. Generate docs/generated/tool-catalog.json unioning SKILL.md frontmatter + MCP roots + worker_registry live regs. Runtime availability reuses connectivity.py endpoint_online() cache (single source of truth). Registry-stale tools flagged by CI before commit (LINT-302). Catalog-first fail-closed tool selection. Consensus: 5/7 subagent types. Foundation: ECC 2.0 fragmentation-detection precedent (docs/v2/ D21).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_88b5a01e9a0a -->
- Fable-5 Doc Layering: ADR pointer sync 100% (sync-docs-v2-pointers.sh --check green, LINT-600). Zero hand-edits to generated Layer-1 pointers. Cite D-number not ADR-NNN in skills (LINT-601). Skill wrapper canonical-path pointers resolve + fresh within N days of referencing CLAUDE.md sections. Never hand-edit LESSONS.md generated by learn.py; use learn.py pipeline for all new entries. Consensus: 5/7 subagent types. Establishes two-repo grounding mechanically via repo_hygiene.py drift detection.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_b61173e76937 -->
- Fable-5 Real-Data-First Validation: Require paired docs/LESSONS.md live-run entry (endpoint, tier, timing via learn.py) as precondition before any Fable-5 v1 pattern is cited as justification in v2 (docs/v2/) planning doc. Cache-correctness diff-harness must pass green on live Fable 5 calls before semantic eval (LINT-700). Cost_guard validated against live budget ceiling (LINT-701). Affinity fail-closed CANCELLED+{policy:true} verified on Win RTX3080 (LINT-702). Consensus: 7/7 subagent types. Gates v2 promotion with measurable, auditable evidence, not assertion.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_27c3b0d5d326 -->
- Fable-5 Skill Creation & Priority Roadmap: Six new consensus skills target Phase 0-1 stabilization: fable5-git-rebase-safety, fable5-tier-based-routing, fable5-mcp-diagnostics, fable5-endpoint-centralization, fable5-tool-cataloging, fable5-doc-layering. Phase 0 unblocks v2 multi-agent workflows (ranks 1-3, medium-small effort, existing foundations). Phase 1 hardens routing/catalog/docs (ranks 4-7, medium-large, triggers Phase 3 real-data-first gate). Phase 2 (concurrency coordination, telemetry aggregation) depends on Phase 0-1 solid. Top upgrade: model-routing-check skill gap (reachability vs actual router resolution), mcp-orchestration diagnostics subsection, hardware-policy timeout unification. Consensus: Architect + Reviewer + General + CodeGuide (highest-tier skill steering group).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_bccc8d38348d -->
- Fable-5 LLM Council session complete (7 subagents, 7 patterns analyzed, 572k tokens): Top 5 skill upgrades (model-routing-check HIGH, mcp-orchestration HIGH, hardware-policy MEDIUM, endpoint-centralization MEDIUM, real-data-first MEDIUM), 6 orama adoption patterns (tiered dataclass, fail-closed gate, dual-track wrappers, live-derived status, zero-fragmentation, MultiLLMRouter decorator), 7 new skills proposed (git-rebase-safety, tier-based-routing, mcp-diagnostics, endpoint-centralization, tool-cataloging, doc-layering, real-data-first-gate). Consensus heat map: 7/7 on routing+safety, 6/7 on diagnostics+centralization+layering+gate, 5/7 on cataloging. All ready for v2 implementation.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_6c5049a223b4 -->
- Live execution validation is non-negotiable for quality gates — static path checks and imports do not catch execution errors (e.g., cost formula mismatches, tier examples that raise exceptions). Re-review must test against real backends.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_feec2a4b9348 -->
- Tier-based routing with flat cost formula 0.001 × est_tokens works across all escalation tiers (no per-tier variation). Each tier must enforce hard 10s timeout via killable background call. Cost gate raises on deny, no silent reroute.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_50a1389e3042 -->
- Parallel subagent fixers work well when scopes are independent (e.g., code example fix vs. contradictions removal). No collision. Both approved on re-review without re-do cycles. Validation must serialize (re-review after all fixers).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_681d8f34406a -->
- ECC submodule upgrade cycle: preserve 3 local-only files via patch + sync script (ecc-submodule-sync.sh restore). Files: frontend-design openai.yaml, .antigravity/ANTIGRAVITY.md, .gemini/ANTIGRAVITY.md. Patch tracked in scripts/git/ecc-local-additions.patch. Always run restore after submodule update.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_00b071f4387d -->
- Canonical repo clones must not exist under duplicate paths like <duplicate>/<OS-specific-home-directory-path>  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_a66da194732a -->
- When a cross-platform infra fix (line endings, encoding, build config) is documented in the wiki, implement it in main the same session — do not leave it as documentation only.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_a3f17e0b8695 -->
- A batch script that switches branches, cherry-picks, or force-pushes against a shared canonical repo checkout must run in an isolated git worktree (git worktree add --detach <path> origin/main), never cd into the live directory other sessions/agents are actively using.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_582410e95c04 -->
- After orama-system's history is rewritten, verify a specific commit's content survived by diffing its tree against origin's current tree (git show <sha>:<path> | diff), not by trusting commit-graph ancestry (merge-base/ahead-behind) or the reanchor_scan classification alone.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_25f7e294cb71 -->
- When a new GPU node (Windows or Mac) joins the LAN fleet, write its onboarding steps as one self-contained downloadable markdown that composes the existing canonical installers (first-run-install.sh, ensure_requirements.ps1) rather than re-deriving install steps inline — keeps a single source of truth and lets the next node's real gaps get fed back as doc edits instead of tribal knowledge.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_1fdca1901294 -->
- graduate.py/learn.py/recall.py/show.py print unicode arrows (→) that crash on Windows' default cp1252 console encoding; force UTF-8 stdout/stderr at each script's entry point (sys.stdout.reconfigure) rather than removing the unicode.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_08fb68bbf805 -->
- Concurrent agents working on the same git repo cause .git/index.lock collisions on every commit, checkout, and stash. The orama-system using-git-worktrees skill exists specifically for this: both agents writing to the same repo → use a worktree. During the 2026-07-08 session, 15+ git operations failed with index.lock errors because another agent was committing to the same orama-system checkout simultaneously. Worktrees would have eliminated every single lock collision.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_7818653bd251 -->
- git filter-repo removes the origin remote as expected behavior after rewriting history. After scrubbing contaminated history with git filter-repo --replace-text, you must git remote add origin <url> again before pushing. The --replace-text option with a rules file (key==>REDACTED) is the correct way to scrub a secret from all blobs across all commits. filter-repo also expires reflogs and prunes unreachable objects automatically.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_c7b4fd2068e4 -->
- .gitattributes CRLF enforcement (eol=crlf on .cmd/.bat files) blocks git rebase and git cherry-pick when old branch commits modified those files with different EOL. The workaround is to temporarily mv .gitattributes .gitattributes.bak AFTER git checkout (since checkout restores .gitattributes from the tree), then restore it before pushing. Even this is fragile: git checkout -B restores .gitattributes, so the mv must happen after checkout, not before. For batch reanchor scripts, use set -uo pipefail without -e so individual branch failures do not abort the entire run.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_3874bb0693e4 -->
- Hardcoded API keys in tracked files are a SECURITY.md violation. The BigModel GLM-5.2 API key was hardcoded in setup-glm52.sh and SKILL.md across 4 tracked files and 8 git commits. Per SECURITY.md: Read keys from environment variables, not source or tracked config. Always use $ENV_VAR or interactive read prompt, never echo literal-key > file. When a key is found in git history, the correct response per SECURITY.md leak-response is: (1) generate+deploy replacement credential, (2) verify it works, (3) revoke the exposed credential, (4) audit usage, (5) remove from active code, (6) git rm --cached if tracked, (7) treat history cleanup as secondary. git filter-repo --replace-text scrubs the key from all blobs but does NOT un-leak it — rotation is mandatory.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_163b4d447595 -->
- ClinePass (Cline Credits via cline-pass/glm-5.2 through api.cline.bot) is the better default for coding tasks over OpenRouter free tier. ClinePass has no rate limits (vs OpenRouter free 50 req/day, 20 RPM), 1M context window, full reasoning + tool loops, and dedicated billing (Cline Credits at app.cline.bot/credits). Use openrouter/free only for lightweight routing/triage. The cline_exec MCP tool (cline_mcp_server.mjs) exposes cline-pass/glm-5.2 to Claude Desktop and Claude Code. Cline CLI auto-refreshes its WorkOS OAuth token (~12 min expiry) so no manual token management is needed. The cline CLI is invoked via: cline task --json --auto-approve true -c dir --thinking medium -P cline-pass -m cline-pass/glm-5.2 --timeout 600 --retries 3  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_7171440c0085 -->
- Cline CLI has no OpenAI completions endpoint — the hub daemon (ws://127.0.0.1:25463/hub) speaks a proprietary WebSocket protocol (GET /v1/models returns 404), and cline --acp speaks ACP not OpenAI completions. The upstream API (api.cline.bot/api/v1/chat/completions) is OpenAI-compatible but fronted by a short-lived WorkOS OAuth token (~12 min expiry) that only the cline CLI auth loop can refresh. Therefore cline-agent in OpenClaw is an agent-level binding not a model-provider binding: the agent uses openrouter/free for lightweight routing and delegates coding to cline CLI via exec/ACP/MCP bridges. This is fundamentally different from codex-agent which binds codex/gpt-5.5 as a native model because codex serve exposes an OpenAI-compatible app-server.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_02ed4a60a9e5 -->
- Never use 'git checkout -- <file>' to undo a small test edit on a file that also has real uncommitted work — it discards ALL uncommitted changes to that file, not just the last edit.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_78ff0ddb7236 -->
- Before removing a config-writing function, grep every consumer of the values it writes across the whole codebase (not just the obvious module) to confirm nothing reads them back — dead-write code can look load-bearing.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_e7c7fa931ecc -->
- On Windows, a script that must call another PowerShell script as a real separate process (not in-process) needs 'powershell.exe -File <script>', not the venv's python.exe — passing a .ps1 to python.exe is a silent no-op, not an error.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_55001efc3c91 -->
- On Windows fresh installs, always probe both the hyphenated and space-separated winget install-directory naming for a given app (e.g. 'LM-Studio' vs 'LM Studio') — winget package IDs and the actual install folder name are not guaranteed to match.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_259ff83a18be -->
- On Windows Python 3.13+, prefer 'netifaces2' over the original 'netifaces' package — the original has no prebuilt wheel past Python 3.11 and fails to build from source without MSVC build tools; netifaces2 is a maintained drop-in fork (same 'import netifaces' API) with prebuilt wheels for current Python.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_d545eb993509 -->
- PowerShell's 'New-ScheduledTaskTrigger -RepetitionDuration ([TimeSpan]::MaxValue)' is rejected by Task Scheduler's XML schema even though it looks like the correct 'run forever' sentinel — use a large-but-bounded duration (e.g. New-TimeSpan -Days 3650) instead.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_21721b435b1f -->
- PowerShell 'Set-StrictMode -Version Latest' turns '(Get-Item -Path Env:VAR -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue).Value' into a hard error when VAR is unset (Get-Item returns $null, .Value on $null throws PropertyNotFoundStrict) — always null-guard the Get-Item result before dereferencing .Value under strict mode.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_55dc6d832a73 -->
- A cached path/config file (like .paths.ps1) written on a broken first run can permanently shadow a later fix unless the code explicitly re-validates the cached value against current reality and force-rewrites the cache when they diverge — just fixing the underlying bug isn't enough if something else still trusts the stale cache.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_4710db82954b -->
- start.ps1 must be run with --lan-peer (not just --no-open) for LAN cross-machine coordination to actually work -- without it, PT/orama/Portal stay bound to localhost only, and the control-plane token never gets synced from PT's persisted state.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_a025b54fc1f0 -->
- The control-plane auth scheme has two independent lanes plus an outbound-only override: PT lane (PT_CONTROL_PLANE_TOKEN env + Perpetua-Tools/.state/control_plane_token file), orama lane (ORAMA_CONTROL_PLANE_TOKEN / ORAMA_CONTROL_PLANE_TOKEN_LOCAL env), and ORAMA_CONTROL_PLANE_TOKEN_PEER (tried first on outbound probes only). auth_mode reports unset/pt_only/orama_only/joint depending on which lanes have candidates; joint means either lane's token unlocks both services.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_7beeab02810a -->
- PowerShell's [Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable(name, value, 'User') writes to the registry but does NOT refresh the CURRENT process's own $env: view -- an already-running shell keeps stale values, and any child process it spawns inherits the parent's stale in-memory env block, not a fresh registry read. Only a genuinely new top-level process (e.g. a fresh Task Scheduler tick) sees the update.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_1888064c81c5 -->
- coord_pulse.ps1 and coord_monitor.ps1 do NOT source .env.local (only start.ps1 does, via scripts/env/load-local.ps1) -- any cross-machine config set only in .env.local (tokens, MAC_IP, timeouts) will not reach these scripts or the Scheduled Task that runs them. Persist such values as actual Windows User-level environment variables, not just .env.local, if coord scripts need them too.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_bff1bbbaa8b6 -->
- Cline instance map (2026-07-08 session): 4 Cline processes run on the Mac. (1) PID 51483 node cline from zsh terminal. (2) PID 51484 .cline main binary 66.8% CPU 619MB active session. (3) PID 44584 .cline --cline-hub-daemon auto ws://127.0.0.1:25463/hub. (4) PID 71165 cline_mcp_server.mjs spawned by Claude Code session 0a13d9d5 as MCP stdio bridge. cline-agent registered in openclaw.json allowAgents but NOT dispatched via gateway. No instances started/stopped in last 3h.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_8eeb77014a20 -->
- When dual_path_dispatch.py fails locally on Mac with 'Context overflow' due to openclaw agent workspace limits, bypass it by compressing the payload and dropping it directly into the peer inbox using lan_peer_assign.py drop.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_25bcf8a9fb7c -->
- The Git repository root for orchestration is orama-system, not the parent OpenClaw directory, and start.sh does not support a --sync flag.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_453443e66fca -->
- For intra-machine multi-agent coordination (concurrent Claude Code/kimi/cline sessions in different git worktrees on the same machine), reuse orchestrator/GossipBus (SQLite FTS5 event log) as a claim/registration board instead of building new infrastructure — it is already intra-machine by design (zero LAN dependency). The only real problem is db-path resolution across worktrees, solved via 'git rev-parse --git-common-dir' (every worktree of the same repo resolves to the same shared .git dir, zero new env vars needed).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_6b25bba72d9f -->
- kimi CLI's -p/--prompt headless mode is incompatible with --yolo (hard error: 'Cannot combine --prompt with --yolo'). -p mode is already non-interactive by definition (no approval loop exists to bypass), so drop --yolo entirely when scripting kimi -p calls.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_0b4968d816f8 -->
- openclaw agent --session-key agent:<id>:<key> requires <id> to already be a REAL, registered agent (openclaw agents list) — an arbitrary/fictional id (e.g. 'review') fails with 'Agent "<id>" no longer exists in configuration'. For a one-off model call without a persistent agent, borrow an EXISTING agent's slot (e.g. agent:main:<ephemeral-key>) with --model as the override; for anything recurring create a real persistent agent via 'openclaw agents add' instead of hand-jq-editing openclaw.json (the live schema uses agentDir/workspace/tools.profile, not the model:{primary} shape an intuitive jq template would produce — mirrors the existing openclaw-cron-add invariant).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_132c7d7a3990 -->
- When a concurrent agent session has uncommitted tracked-file changes in the SAME shared git checkout (not a workt…62189 tokens truncated…idence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_e45608de48c0 -->  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_788e5f27cf28 -->
- Model dispatch security uses a positive allowlist: scripts/check_model_ids.py scans config/models.yml and .env.example only; new model IDs require explicit ALLOWED_MODEL_IDS entry (T2-B). Denylist check_no_hallucinated_models.py is insufficient alone (S7).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_fdae75a42410 -->
- Co-authored-by guards must fail-closed on parsed email: if angle-bracket email is present but not allowlisted, reject immediately — display-name markers (hermes, qwen, claude) never override. Fuzz cases in tests/test_git_attribution_guard.py (T3-B).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_43cca31d78aa -->
- oramaclaw pending conflicts cleared or orphaned by dead transactions must be archived to state_dir/registry/orphan-conflicts/ before removal — never silent delete (T3-C). sweep_orphan_pending runs on ControlStore.open.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_c3af76efdbee -->
- Security and API fixes must trace the full data path from source to sink — a helper fix (e.g. _canonical_endpoint on return paths) does not protect import-time constants, module-level env reads, or unvalidated JSON consumers that bypass the helper.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_ff614271268e -->
- Dream-cycle salience scoring must compare UTC-aware timestamps — episodic entries use +00:00 offsets; naive datetime.now() minus aware fromisoformat raises TypeError and blocks auto_dream.py.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_5cf4ebfacd65 -->
- PR descriptions and summaries are append-only historical records: never replace the original purpose with a follow-up job summary. When new work lands (CodeRabbit fixes, merge resolutions, CI notes), add a ## Follow-up section below the existing scope — same rule as LESSONS.md and integrative-merge doctrine.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_2a6a6a3c9aa2 -->
- Merge-conflict synthesize mode: when both branches changed the same region for valid different reasons, blend both — do not pick one side wholesale. Example PR #158: keep ipaddress-based _validate_endpoint_host from security-hardening AND locality/canonical-loopback tests from #157.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_9de6d9ecd62c -->
- After a main history rewrite, never classify branches by merge-base exit 1 or ahead/behind counts alone — use scripts/git/reanchor_scan.sh plus git cherry -v; merge-base failure often means rewrite boundary not orphan work.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_b0b5ec22ca15 -->
- Before destructive git ops (rebase all, branch delete, history surgery), append a markdown branch catalog to .agent/memory/working/BRANCH_CATALOG_COMPLETE_<date>.md using reanchor_scan output — read-only snapshot for triage.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_aad4ecc7a01f -->
- Git sticky routing before branch work: git-history-surgery SKILL for rewrite/reanchor; reanchor-after-rewrite.md for Case A/B twins; using-git-worktrees for worktree safety; windows-powershell-runtime-bootstrap before Win fetch/rebase/push; platform-line-endings-turf for .cmd CRLF (blocks macOS checkout).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_08c6136efd54 -->
- RC-1 CommandCenter :33 empty-string fallback is sufficiently covered by commandCenterState.test.ts unit tests; full CommandCenter.test.tsx component test is optional when pure helpers are extracted and tested.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_a60b91720b3b -->
- When exploratory scratch PR (#117 vitest) and integration PR (#118 dev-recalib) overlap, fold scratch into integration or close scratch after merge — scratch branches can pass CI without exercising pnpm test if deps only exist on integration branch.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_d33b20335653 -->
- Vitest nav smoke tests: click Composer/Runs/Artifacts nav buttons and assert exclusive panel chrome — e.g. runs page has View All Runs but not Swarm Composer/Launch Swarm. File: web/src/features/command-center/CommandCenter.test.tsx.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_e896d6bbfe86 -->
- Tracked skill files with literal workstation tree paths fail no-workstation-paths / LINT-006 on edit — use $(git rev-parse --show-toplevel) or repo-relative examples in using-git-worktrees and similar skills.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_84a7b39131f4 -->
- Overlapping Windows CRLF turf PRs on platform/windows/gstack-brain-sync.cmd (#116, #117, #118) require coordinated merge order — land #116 (EOL normalize) before Vitest/dev-recalib integration or expect EOL conflict churn.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_4afc1829aa8f -->
- Playwright E2E for orama web/ operator console is explicitly deferred until the Vitest RC-1 gate merges to main — tracked in docs/TDD.md post-gate backlog, not RC-1 minimum scope.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_5748f1152f3b -->
- After rewrite-boundary branch triage, dry cherry-pick onto current origin/main: if the patch applies empty or changes are already on main, delete the branch — do not open a PR (CI/pt71 cluster, routing branches session 2026-06-27).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_0ec0c8a452f8 -->
- gbrain sync may block sync.last_commit on a single bad SKILL.md parse — use gbrain sync --skip-failed to acknowledge pre-existing failures and advance checkpoint after large orama doc imports.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_8d89a2d99246 -->
- RC-1 Vitest operator-console gate (16 tests, 5 files, CI web-test job) is complete on feat/vitest-tdd-gate-scratch but not on origin/main until PR #118 merges — main web/package.json still has no vitest script.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_a82998ce486c -->
- Append a Progress status section to docs/testing/*.tdd.md evidence reports before merge — captures branch-vs-main state, open PRs, and backlog so triage survives branch cleanup.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_5afa74f29ac5 -->
- TDD commit-msg gate: scripts/git/check_tdd_commit.sh blocks staged web/src production *.ts(x) without a staged *.test.ts(x) in the same commit unless the message contains tdd-skip: <reason>. Wired from .githooks/commit-msg via install-local-hooks.sh — not pre-commit (message unavailable there).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_4dc1a87af447 -->
- Ship related changes in logical commit batches for review: (1) tests, (2) hook/scripts, (3) canonical docs, (4) skill cross-refs — used on feat/vitest-tdd-gate-scratch 2026-06-27 (4 commits).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_8c78db91c92d -->
- macOS ships bash 3.2 as /bin/bash — it lacks mapfile/readarray. scripts/git/*.sh hook scripts must use while IFS= read -r loops. Symptom: check_tdd_commit.sh exit 127 mapfile: command not found. Canonical ref: orama git-history-surgery/references/bash-32-git-script-portability.md.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_b3c5ceda54d8 -->
- Block agentic-stack upstream Brain integration in .agent until brain_bridge.py supports both Gbrain and codejunkie99/brain — Gbrain via gstack is canonical for PT-orama RAG  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_b999c0e59e93 -->
- ~/.gbrain/config.json must have a 'database_url' key explicitly — ~/.gbrain/.env is NOT auto-sourced in non-interactive shells (Claude Code agents, launchd background jobs). Export the URL from .env into config.json directly, or gbrain fails with 'database_url is missing from config' in every non-interactive context.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_eff7dea7da5a -->
- SKILL.md YAML frontmatter must be the first bytes of the file — place lint-ignore HTML comments after the closing frontmatter delimiter  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_1974c4e4950b -->
- patch_models_yml must skip loopback win_ip the same way patch_devices_yml does — never persist localhost into shared YAML defaults  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_fc2ed83116c7 -->
- Pre-v2 security hardening (v1.1.1.0): all Linux-runnable tiers ship on cursor/security-hardening-pre-v2-c4ae in both repos; Mac/Win live E2E (start.sh, Ollama probes, LM_STUDIO_WIN_ENDPOINTS, hardware-policy harness, keychain) and T5 freeze tags stay blocked until tomorrow on real machines. Plan: orama-system/docs/plans/2026-06-27-security-hardening-pre-v2.md — platform schedule table marks each tier.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_6fea42d29309 -->
- When git pull is blocked by local commits, use git pull --rebase origin main. If the local commit content is already upstream (e.g. a duplicate episodic memory entry), rebase drops it silently with 'dropping...patch contents already upstream' — this is correct, not data loss.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_751e8dc70ef7 -->
- Hermes thin wrappers: four required (council, review, delegate, hardware-policy); lesson-mining is optional via --include-optional only — orama-system has no Perpetua-Tools dependency for graduation  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_b805c620f413 -->
- v2 oramasys/agate repo will own model_hardware_policy.yml schema, hardware_policy API, and hardware_policy_cli — perpetua-core imports agate; PT v1 paths are migration source only  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_b14332058198 -->
- LINT-006 flags OS-specific home-directory path forms and $HOME patterns even inside policy docs that WARN against them — use placeholder notation abstract home-directory placeholders or OS-specific home-directory path in ALL docs explaining what not to do.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_9eff6846be84 -->
- Mac—”Win cross-harness E2E verification requires an actual curl probe: curl -s --max-time 5 http://${WIN_IP}:1234/v1/models — the start.sh --hardware-policy flag only validates openclaw.json config, it does NOT probe the live Win LM Studio endpoint.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_f0fd779ad46e -->
- routing.json consumed by alphaclaw_bootstrap must pass jsonschema validation and RFC-1918 hostname allowlist before build_openclaw_config — never trust filesystem JSON without _validate_pt_state (T1-A).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_089a5ca9360f -->
- setup_macos.py step_mac_agent_thinking must guard against string model shorthand — openclaw.json agents may use 'model': '<id>' (string) instead of {"primary": ...}. Guard with isinstance(_model, str): return before calling .get('primary'). Without guard: AttributeError crashes start.sh --status silently.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_f8078a0c32c7 -->
- All .agent/memory writers must call sanitize_tracked_path_leaks() from path_hygiene.py before persisting strings — episodic log_execution, learn.py, graduate.py, and review queue renderer. Legacy rows: run scrub_memory_paths.py then re-render LESSONS.md; never hand-edit derived markdown to hide paths.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_26f57e7dab29 -->
- T5 tagging procedure: git tag v1.1.1 -m '<message>' then git push origin v1.1.1 in BOTH repos in the same session. No version bump needed if pyproject.toml/\_version.py already reflect the target version. Tag only after ALL E2E gates pass (Mac E2E + Win E2E + Mac—’Win cross-probe).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_c13eabe6ea48 -->
- agentic-stack lives at vendor/agentic-stack in Perpetua-Tools like ecc-tools — orama start.sh symlinks lib/shared/agentic_stack from PT vendor path  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_4b66551cb16b -->
- discover.py must split hash endpoints (LAN IP) from runtime endpoints (localhost on Windows) — never mutate endpoints win to LAN IP before patch_openclaw_json or lmstudio-win breaks  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_f1585589cce5 -->
- orama-system TDD gate (pre-commit check for adjacent *.test.ts(x) when web/src/ production files change) fires on merge resolution commits even when no new code was authored. Override with 'tdd-skip: <reason>' in the git commit message body — the commit-msg hook reads this line and bypasses the TDD requirement.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_8b5f611bc3e4 -->
- At Mac session start after a Win session, always read REVIEW_QUEUE.md FIRST — the Win session completes tasks that Mac thought were pending. Never repeat work the Win session already did.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_9bb87bbd0fc6 -->
- After vendor/agentic-stack submodule init run install-agentic-stack.sh then agentic-stack upgrade --dry-run before harmonizing PT .agent/ — union-merge only, never commit into vendor/  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_dd349e043542 -->
- When adding Windows .cmd shims in Python always write CRLF bytes and add gitattributes eol=crlf — LF-only batch files fail silently under cmd.exe per PR #108 gstack-brain-sync.cmd incident  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_391190279f2b -->
- xAI Grok Build (Elon Musk) installs a bare agent CLI at ~/.grok/bin/agent — it is NOT Cursor. PATH collision: scripts invoking agent without a path hit Grok TUI instead of cursor-agent. Always call cursor-agent explicitly and document both binaries in harness skills.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_9121a324a3af -->
- git add --renormalize with 'text eol=crlf' in .gitattributes can store raw CRLF bytes in the blob on macOS, creating a phantom git diff where cmp shows working-tree and HEAD blob are byte-identical yet git diff HEAD shows 69+ lines changed. Fix: write LF content to file, git add (normalizes to LF blob), commit locally, then git pull --rebase drops it as 'patch already upstream'.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_c07c752c1625 -->
- PR landing in orama-system and Perpetua-Tools always uses squash-merge (gh pr merge --squash). When a PR shows CONFLICTING/DIRTY on GitHub: (1) git merge origin/main locally, (2) resolve conflicts additively (union both sides), (3) push branch, (4) gh pr merge --squash. Never regular merge commit; never rebase-merge. squash keeps main history linear.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_3be801c8b5c2 -->
- SSRF validation must convert IPv4-mapped IPv6 addresses before checking is_link_local: if isinstance(addr, ipaddress.IPv6Address) and addr.ipv4_mapped is not None: addr = addr.ipv4_mapped. Without this, ::ffff:169.254.x.x passes ipv6.is_link_local=False check even though the underlying IPv4 address is link-local. Fixed in PT PR #154 alphaclaw_bootstrap.py.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_7ab7b4874616 -->
- macOS Keychain service names for OpenClaw: 'openclaw.gemini-main-apikey' (->OPENCLAW_MODELS_PROVIDERS_GEMINI_MAIN_APIKEY), 'openclaw.gemini-fallback-apikey' (->OPENCLAW_MODELS_PROVIDERS_GEMINI_FALLBACK_APIKEY), 'openclaw.telegram-bot-token' (->OPENCLAW_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN), 'openclaw.gateway-auth-token' (->OPENCLAW_GATEWAY_AUTH_TOKEN). Store: printf '%s' VALUE | bash $OPENCLAW_ROOT/orama-system/scripts/openclaw/store_keychain_secret.sh <service>. Load at startup: source $OPENCLAW_ROOT/orama-system/scripts/openclaw/load_keychain_secrets.sh  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_540dd3b72da8 -->
- Portal peer-file 404: pull orama 86c90bc+, restart --lan-peer; peer-stream needs response_class=StreamingResponse  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_f0cc7a9174c4 -->
- Post-commit sync: git fetch --prune, pull --rebase origin main, push, verify HEAD equals origin/main in both repos  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_16519c40fa2d -->
- LM Studio loads only one model per instance; probe state=loaded via /api/v0/models before dispatch  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_e202659006c6 -->
- Windows partner CLIs use parametric LOCALAPPDATA and USERPROFILE paths; bootstrap with ensure-partner-cli-paths.ps1  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_b34907073b3a -->
- Codex CLI v0.142.x uses three dispatch profiles: fanout (codex exec -C repo -s workspace-write --dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox), bounded (exec workspace-write only), interactive (--sandbox danger-full-access --ask-for-approval never). Legacy --approval-mode removed in 0.140+. Resolve repo root and Codex binary at runtime; never hardcode host paths in prompts.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_6d77a1268be4 -->
- Hermes skill absorption: redirect stubs and .agents wrappers stay thin; canonical bodies live only in bin/orama-system/skills. perpetua-hardware in .agents targets hardware-affinity-gate (orama methodology); PT runtime SSoT stays in Perpetua-Tools policy YAML. Archive llm-council-orchestration is SUPERSEDED by pt-orama-council; Win LM Studio coder is qwen3.5-27b-claude-4.6-opus-reasoning-distilled-v2 not invented Qwen 3.6 Coder.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_a1a4ef674212 -->
- Hermes dispatch envelope: L3 skill_id+args+agent_id; L2 adds orama_system_root, executor_id, optional transport partner profile; L0 OpenClaw core result plus optional Hermes extensions  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_7423114e6a62 -->
- Mac↔Win cross-harness E2E verification requires an actual curl probe: curl -s --max-time 5 http://${WIN_IP}:1234/v1/models — the start.sh --hardware-policy flag only validates openclaw.json config, it does NOT probe the live Win LM Studio endpoint.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_0b7048649895 -->
- T5 tagging procedure: git tag v1.1.1 -m '<message>' then git push origin v1.1.1 in BOTH repos in the same session. No version bump needed if pyproject.toml/\_version.py already reflect the target version. Tag only after ALL E2E gates pass (Mac E2E + Win E2E + Mac→Win cross-probe).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_0c7879ce4d23 -->
- When git commit output is piped through '| tail -N', a commit failure can look like success because the error message is truncated. Always check 'git status --short' after a commit that used tail-truncated output to confirm the file is no longer staged.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_e47e41408685 -->
- Mac and Win orama-system installs self-talk over LAN via HTTP only: peer IP from last_discovery.json, inference via LM Studio /v1/models, control-plane via portal /health and /api/status after PORTAL_BIND_LAN=1 and shared ORAMA_CONTROL_PLANE_TOKEN on both hosts — use probe_lan_peer.py or Hermes /lan-peer-self-talk; no SSH and no second RPC layer  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_1b71b05f5da4 -->
- Windows start.ps1 must parse bash-style flags from args (--no-open --stop --status) because PowerShell switch params reject --kebab-case; never assign to dollar-pid — use listenerPid — PID is a read-only automatic variable  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_596a27db942f -->
- Windows orama uvicorn launch must match start.sh: orama_system.api_server and portal_server modules with PYTHONPATH repo src and root — bare api_server colon app fails silently  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_7a83b497caa3 -->
- PowerShell ProcessStartInfo EnvironmentVariables is a StringDictionary — use ContainsKey not Contains or Start-Service throws before uvicorn launches  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_3dc021959a2b -->
- After start.ps1 rehab on Windows verify PT 8000 orama 8001 Portal 8002 UP via start.ps1 --status before LAN peer probe  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_94f81462f70f -->
- Windows LAN peer self-talk succeeds when .env.local sets PORTAL_BIND_LAN=1 and ORAMA_BIND_LAN=1, start.ps1 is launched via powershell.exe -File with LF line endings, and last_discovery.json is current  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_91b002661a70 -->
- NEVER run git clean -fd or git clean -fdx without backup or stash  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_2b982fd6f356 -->
- If the only goal is 'worktree matches remote', use git restore --staged --worktree . and manually remove confirmed files; do not destroy untracked files first  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_200da8d8a5d3 -->
- LAN peer co-orchestration uses markdown file drops via lan_peer_assign.py and POST /api/peer-file to ~/.openclaw/state/lan_peer/inbox/. Fan-out manifest JSON splits topics by assignee mac or win. WS and SSE are for heartbeat and probe only; large autoresearch payloads are plain files.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_81cce2bd3ca0 -->
- lan_peer_assign.py resolves orama repo root as Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4], not parents[5]. Local inbox import requires orama_system on sys.path from repo/src. parents[5] pointed at OpenClaw parent and caused ModuleNotFoundError.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_b709ac30725e -->
- lan_peer_assign fanout continues on per-item peer drop failures and returns status partial with exit code 1 when any remote drop fails while local assignments succeed. Mac can proceed while Win catches up on git pull and portal restart.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_7ed642833052 -->
- On Win, Mac-to-Win assignments arrive in the local lan_peer inbox. Win operators read inbound files with lan_peer_assign.py list and read --name without --peer. Inbound peer files are already local after Mac drop --peer.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_939d7505ba57 -->
- Win co-orchestrator replies use lan_peer_assign.py drop --peer --file with assignee mac. Mac reads Win deliverables via list --peer and read --peer --name. Standard deliverables: gpu-results.md, win-code-review.md, win-self-improve-runtime-results.md.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_47ed24d9f626 -->
- Mac must git pull orama-system main at or after 9f89051 and restart with ./start.sh --lan-peer --no-open before /api/peer-file accepts Win drops. portal-health can PASS while peer-file returns 404 on an old portal process.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_d21dd50f74e8 -->
- LAN peer joint auth mode accepts PT lane token from Perpetua-Tools .state/control_plane_token plus orama env lanes. probe_lan_peer.py reports auth_mode joint when both lanes are configured; either key unlocks portal-status on probes.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_646238f813fd -->
- On Mac when Ollama is warm and LM Studio is passive-only, route Mac co-orchestrator and subagent inference through ollama-mac at localhost:11434. LM Studio :1234 may still answer peer-lmstudio probes with a model catalog but must not be the primary execution path unless hardware policy requires MLX on that port.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_c0b14f576f10 -->
- Tests and docs must not embed $HOME/ or other workstation-specific absolute paths — repo_hygiene LINT-006 blocks them; use <local-temp> placeholders or repo-relative paths in fixtures.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_1a80ad5495a8 -->
- When ws-peer probe PASS is bidirectional between Mac and Win portal, L3 file inbox co-orchestration is fully operational. Mac uses OpenClaw plus Ollama warm; Win uses Hermes plus LM Studio 27B. Coordinate only via lan_peer_assign file drops, not remote agent RPC.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_cada6ad563e1 -->
- Windows Portal http://localhost:8002/ Internal Server Error on dashboard root: pull orama-system main at or after 435d27a and restart start.ps1 --lan-peer. Fix unwraps redacted agents dict in portal_server _render_html; /health may PASS while / returns 500 on stale portal process.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_717ca245eba5 -->
- Portal / returned HTTP 500 after restart if portal_server is not updated first: rerun start.ps1 --lan-peer after pulling latest origin/main  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_68d80e80b24c -->
- Co-orchestration mutations use subagent branches named subagent/role/topic from origin/main; coordination stays on main via file inbox.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_9a279b0dbb6e -->
- Policy docs that explain forbidden path patterns must use placeholder notation OS-specific home-directory paths or OS-specific home-directory path form — never literal OS-specific home-directory segments or the linter will fail even on warning docs  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_8efa642ace57 -->
- portal-health PASS + peer-lmstudio PASS does NOT mean dispatch authorized — POST to /api/peer-file returns HTTP 401 without openclaw.gateway-auth-token in Keychain (portal-status SKIP = token absent). Always check auth layer separately from reachability layer.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_f3d1353a6eea -->
- New markdown files over 200 lines trigger a WARNING (not a block) from orama repo_hygiene pre-commit — commit still succeeds. Consider offloading details to references/ or sub-skills for files approaching this size.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_868883ea42fe -->
- Cross-harness wiring docs belong in orama-system/docs/how-to/ — not inside skill references/ directories (those are skill-internal) and not in docs/wiki/ (wiki is concept-level). how-to/ is for operational runbooks agents and humans self-reference.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_86311090f24f -->
- openclaw.json's .cron.jobs schema is NOT the raw shape shown in some skill docs (id/agentId/schedule.type/schedule.value/prompt) — hand-editing that shape breaks config validation silently until 'openclaw config validate' or 'openclaw status' surfaces 'cron: Invalid input'. Always use 'openclaw cron add --agent X --every/--cron/--at ... --message ...' CLI instead of jq-editing openclaw.json directly.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_2612b25426c9 -->
- This machine's OpenClaw launchd label is ai.openclaw.gateway (not com.openclaw.gateway as some skill docs assume) on port 18789 (not 7331), and exposes a WebSocket gateway (ws://) not a plain HTTP /health route — curl to /health will always fail there. Use 'openclaw status' or 'openclaw gateway probe' to check liveness, not curl.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_d0526e50ed2a -->
- Locked D2–D5 operational decisions remain active independently of the revised D1 versioning lesson: keep runtime-agent configuration distinct from methodology-agent configuration; retain the integration feature scope and supported platform/install coverage; retain documented authentication precedence and its non-interactive fallback; and retain documentation for both command-line and supervisor HTTP entrypoints. Do not reopen these decisions without an explicit human override.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_5869d08b2179 -->

### 2026-07

- Windows Task Scheduler rejects [TimeSpan]::MaxValue as a RepetitionDuration - it serializes to P99999999DT23H59M59S which the XML schema rejects  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_c709f609c537 -->
- Hermes user-local skills live under HERMES_HOME/skills/<category>/<name>/SKILL.md, not ~/.hermes/skills - HERMES_HOME can be a custom env var pointing elsewhere on Windows  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ac7b0254bada -->
- When a git branch has diverged from origin with unrelated concurrent commits, reset --hard to origin tip and re-apply only your own deltas as fresh edits, rather than fighting an interactive rebase across duplicate/superseded history  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_3060e4382900 -->
- A file showing 'M' in git status/diff with every line changed but zero semantic diff (confirmed via diff with tr -d carriage-return) is EOL-attribute false-dirty, not a real edit - fix once with git add <file> to renormalize, never git checkout-- which can silently discard real changes if you're wrong  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_f37d3322a020 -->
- PT .agent memory pipeline had unencoded open() calls scattered across graduate.py, render_lessons.py, learn.py beyond the two files fixed earlier (promote.py, auto_dream.py) - the UTF-8 codec bug on Windows cp1252 recurs at every new read site until the whole call chain is audited, not just the entry point  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_3afc37f1ec96 -->
- scripts/ensure_requirements.ps1 (PowerShell) must stay PowerShell 5.1 compatible on Windows: no ?? null-coalescing operator, no 3+ positional-arg Join-Path calls, and UTF-8 files with em-dashes in double-quoted strings need a BOM  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_883e26e451c5 -->
- PowerShell's Set-Content -Encoding UTF8 writes a UTF-8 BOM. When Python reads that same file with open(path, encoding='utf-8') or Path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'), json.loads silently fails on the BOM byte and callers that catch the exception broadly (returning a default like paused=False) get a wrong answer with no visible error - use encoding='utf-8-sig' when reading any file a PowerShell script wrote with Set-Content -Encoding UTF8  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_21bcbcb96cee -->
- Before deleting any uncommitted untracked file discovered mid-session as a suspected side-effect, check git log --all -- <path> and cross-reference against other files that import/reference/test it (e.g. a matching tests/test_X.py) - an untracked .py file with a matching test suite is very likely another agent's real in-progress work, not build noise, even if it appeared alongside genuine bootstrap-script side effects in the same git status  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_349ea348289d -->
- ClinePass AutoResearcher fallback (scripts/cline_autoresearcher.py in orama-system) + parallel dispatch race (_dispatch_race in spawn_agents.py) + solo-mode resilience (lan_peer_session.py, 10x-fail -> demote -> 15min recheck) were ALL already implemented and merged to orama main before this session started work on them — verified via git log + live status check (solo_mode:true, failure_count:65, retry_seconds:900) rather than re-implemented from scratch.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_eb25c5ce22ff -->
- A run_in_background git push reporting 'completed (exit code 0)' does NOT prove main is clean or the remote matches — the exit code can come from an early stage of a chained command while a later 'git pull --rebase' or 'git commit' stalls interactively (e.g. mid-rebase waiting on rebase --continue, or a corrupted commit-msg hook). Always follow a backgrounded push with an explicit 'git status --short --branch' check before reporting sync as done.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_8141cd307a70 -->
- A whole-file git conflict (markers spanning nearly the entire file) usually means one side substantially rewrote the file while the other commit never intended to touch it at all — check 'git show <incoming-commit> -- <file>' first; if the incoming commit's own diff is empty for that file, its tree snapshot is just stale carry-through, and the correct resolution is almost always --ours (the side with the real, intentional changes), not a line-by-line merge.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_c4e03e375efe -->
- When 'git rebase --continue' repeats 'You must edit all merge conflicts and then mark them as resolved using git add' even though git status shows zero unmerged paths and 'git ls-files -u' is empty, the real blocker is downstream in a git hook (commit-msg/pre-commit) failing silently — run 'git commit --no-edit' directly to surface the hook's actual stderr instead of re-reading the stale rebase message.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_566df2b0e921 -->
- MCP singleton pattern: when N registrations point to the same server, run ONE backend daemon on a Unix socket and have each registration wrapper probe the socket, start the daemon (nohup, detached) if dead, then bridge its own stdio via Python threading. No socat dependency.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_6cff80879069 -->
- JSON-RPC multiplexing over a single stdio backend: mangle request IDs to '{client_id}:{original_id}' before forwarding; on responses, split by ':' to demux back to the originating client. Cache initialize capabilities and tools/list results so subsequent clients are served from cache without hitting the backend.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_7065d5d02904 -->
- LINT-006 + .mcp.json args: JSON does not expand shell variables, so a literal $HOME in a JSON args array is NOT expanded by the MCP launcher. To avoid committing $HOME/... paths, use bash -c indirection: ["bash","-c","exec bash \"$HOME/path/to/wrapper.sh\""]. The outer bash -c spawns with a real shell that expands $HOME before exec-ing the target.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ff0b077fe4b0 -->
- MCP daemon startup: after launching a background daemon (nohup, detached), probe the Unix socket in a loop with 0.1s sleeps and a hard ceiling (50 iterations = 5s). Fail with a JSON-RPC error response to stderr if the socket never becomes connectable. Do not block stdin or use sleep chains.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_64f76b25fa5c -->
- .agent/ root is NOT a drop zone for arbitrary files. The only recognized subdirectories are memory/, skills/, protocols/, tools/. Policy contracts belong in protocols/; lessons must go through learn.py -> graduate.py -> render pipeline into memory/semantic/LESSONS.md — never as a hand-edited .agent/lessons.md at root. Committing files to .agent/ without reading AGENTS.md is the root anti-pattern.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_0c17b0718745 -->
- NEVER write lessons by hand-editing any file in .agent/. The pipeline is: python3 .agent/tools/learn.py '<claim>' --rationale '...' (stages + graduates + re-renders memory/semantic/LESSONS.md automatically). Creating a parallel .agent/lessons.md or .agent/memory/semantic/LESSONS.md by hand defeats the candidate review system and salience decay.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_8e7d657cb5bb -->
- Numbered plan docs require index verification: before creating any numbered doc in orama-system/docs/v2/*, read docs/v2/README.md first, use its 'Next free slot' as the coordination lock, create at that slot, then update the README spec tree and advance 'Next free slot' in the same pass. If a wrong-numbered file was created, move content to the correct slot and delete the wrong file.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_2aafbcc58baa -->
- Repo source of truth beats review projections: margin/PR-comments/Cursor-handoff-docs/chat-summaries are review surfaces, not sources of truth. Workflow: update the repo file first → publish the rendered review copy second → fold review comments back into the repo file → reuse the same Margin doc when revising → never treat Margin comments as approval.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_f48689c134df -->
- SKILL.md authoring: SKILL.md is the orchestrator, not the encyclopedia. New skills must target <=200 lines; existing/exceptional skills <=500 lines. Long templates, examples, anti-patterns, detailed workflows, and eval checklists belong in modular files under instructions/, examples/, references/, templates/, scripts/, eval/. Read the repo skill architecture guide before changing authoring rules.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_984e40eaeedb -->
- Verify branch and PR state before narration: do not narrate that a commit, PR, or file is on main without verifying current branch/file state. Required pattern: fetch the PR or file from the target branch → compare actual changed files against the stated plan → state what is landed / only documented / still missing → do not close/reset/delete branches until changes are confirmed on main.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_0290bab88800 -->
- AlphaClaw is controlled through Perpetua: do not edit the AlphaClaw fork directly during cross-repo endpoint, OpenClaw, or v2 planning work. Represent AlphaClaw only as a downstream controlled surface unless the user explicitly changes ownership instructions.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_aa2344879e3b -->
- Dry-run before long-running or cross-repo execution: for long-running goals, autonomous loops, plugin installs, GPU work, external LLM usage, or cross-repo changes — run/produce a dry-run plan first showing affected files and commands, classify risks and ownership, avoid side effects until reviewed. Dry-run must not call paid/external LLMs, install plugins, touch GPUs, mutate AlphaClaw, or mutate repos beyond a plan/report file.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_577328d008b7 -->
- FAILURE in skill-absorption-map: hermes-harness should absorb hermes-agent and pt-orama-harness-integration; perpetua-hardware should absorb local-inference  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.7 evidence=2 id=lesson_bb70a6833f36 -->
- Cross-machine knowledge transfer pathway: Win AutoResearcher (start.ps1 / Hermes) generates episodic evidence via skills like skill-absorption-map → auto_dream.py clusters them into candidates in PT .agent/memory → candidates persist across dream cycles until a human reviews on Mac. This is how Hermes self-assessments, Win session diagnostics, and AutoResearcher findings arrive in the shared portable brain. The pathway is: Win skill/session → AGENT_LEARNINGS.jsonl (evidence_ids) → auto_dream staging → REVIEW_QUEUE.md → human graduate/reject on Mac.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_26cc05980836 -->
- skill-absorption-map 'FAILURE' label means unabsorbed skill clusters were found — it is a diagnostic output, NOT a system error. The map identifies skills that should be consolidated into parent harnesses. Treat FAILURE candidates as architecture TODOs: they specify which child skills should be absorbed into which harness (e.g. hermes-harness absorbs hermes-agent + pt-orama-harness-integration; perpetua-hardware absorbs local-inference). Act on them as refactoring targets, not bug reports.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_31a5240e8945 -->
- ~~TO THE AGENT WHO WROTE .agent/endpoint-policy-contract.yml (2026-06-29, the repo's approved identity <<private email identity>>, H6/wiring session): You knew AGENTS.md existed — you listed it in required_local_files. That is not the same as reading it. Protocol contracts belong in .agent/protocols/ alongside permissions.md, delegation.md, and path-hygiene.md. The correct move: before creating ANY file in .agent/, open .agent/AGENTS.md and read the structure section. The directory structure is explicit: memory/, skills/, protocols/, tools/. If your content does not fit one of those four subdirectories, question whether it belongs in .agent/ at all.~~  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_9b792cfa1924 superseded_by=lesson_acaa4ff3a02a -->
- ~~TO THE AGENT WHO WROTE .agent/lessons.md (2026-07-04 07:15, the repo's approved identity <<redacted-private-identity>>, AutoResearcher session): The correct move to record lessons is exactly one command: python3 .agent/tools/learn.py '<your claim>' --rationale '<why this holds>'. That stages, graduates, and re-renders memory/semantic/LESSONS.md automatically. Do NOT create any markdown file by hand in .agent/ — not at root, not anywhere. .agent/lessons.md does not exist in the AGENTS.md structure; nothing reads it; you created a phantom file. Also: your git identity (<redacted-private-identity>) is not in the canonical allowlist — use the repo's approved identity <<private email identity>> or the repo's approved identity <<private email identity>>.~~  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_23e51efcde2f superseded_by=lesson_c3b24dc506a6 -->
- Listing a file as a dependency or required_local_file is NOT the same as reading it. If .agent/AGENTS.md appears in your output (in a contract, a plan, a checklist), that is a signal you need to open and read it — not just acknowledge its existence. The same applies to any convention-defining file (CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, README.md). Cargo-culting a filename into a list while skipping its content is how AFRP Trigger 3 failures happen.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_b429cb46d40d -->
- GLM-5.2 ultimate fallback pathway: when ClinePass, LM Studio, Ollama, and all other primary execution pathways are blocked or unavailable, agents queue tasks for reliable execution via GLM-5.2. Setup: one-time bootstrap sourcing ~/.openclaw/.env.glm52 (api_key from ~/.openclaw/secrets/glm52-api-key + endpoint https://open.bigmodel.cn/api/paas/v4/chat/completions). Fallback chain: ClinePass → LM Studio → Ollama → OpenAI/GPT → **GLM-5.2 (always works)**. Health check: curl GLM-5.2 endpoint, verify HTTP 200 with reasoning tokens. No manual intervention needed after bootstrap — fallback is transparent to agents.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_7c7bb44e4ece -->
- Orchestration layer wiring for GLM-5.2: start.sh (Mac) and start.ps1 (Windows) both source ~/.openclaw/.env.glm52 at bootstrap and print fallback status prominently after services startup. Variables: $GLM52_API_KEY, $GLM52_ENDPOINT. Both scripts check availability and log whether fallback is ACTIVE or NOT AVAILABLE. This is the entry point where all agent tasks can discover and use GLM-5.2 when needed.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ff1d6f8538f4 -->
- 10 coding/routing skills wired with GLM-5.2 fallback sourcing: (1-3 workspace: glm52-fallback, orama-system, orama-orchestrator), (4-7 thin wrappers: code-review, git-history-surgery, orama-afrp, perpetua-config), (8-10 gstack full-featured: investigate, devex-review, benchmark-models). Each skill includes preamble or frontmatter: 'source ~/.openclaw/.env.glm52 && echo GLM-5.2 Fallback Activated'. Pattern: workspace skills get an inline section; thin wrappers get a section after Before Use; gstack skills get a preamble-init field in frontmatter. This ensures every skill activation loads the fallback environment.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_abd77882c935 -->
- Hermes-Harness integration with GLM-5.2: both orama-system (canonical) and PT (thin wrapper) hermes-harness skills now document that when LM Studio or other Hermes-accessible models are unavailable on Windows, the task queue automatically falls back to GLM-5.2. This is wired by start.ps1 (Windows orchestrator). No additional setup beyond the one-time Mac-side bootstrap. Hermes is the operator shell for ECC workflows; GLM-5.2 ensures that shell can execute any queued task even when primary models fail.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_f2c641c82ad2 -->
- GLM-5.2 safe defaults and error recovery: (1) API key in secrets, environment file auto-sourced, health check with 10s connect + 120s max timeout + 3 retries.
