- ~~Recurring miss: I keep writing literal workstation paths (workspace-root shorthand path, a synced workspace tree) into TRACKED docs, then only fix them after the no-workstation-paths hook / LINT-006 blocks me. Write paths abstractly the FIRST time: $OPENCLAW_ROOT / $REPO_ROOT / repo-relative (../../<repo>/) / username-containing placeholders. Check the path-hygiene rule BEFORE writing a doc, not after the block.~~  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_5b891b07e193 superseded_by=lesson_94e345fe9e47 -->
- When merging branches that both append to .agent memory (lessons.jsonl, episodic/AGENT_LEARNINGS.jsonl), resolve conflicts by UNION — keep both sides, dedup by lesson 'id' (jsonl) or by line (episodic) — then RE-RENDER LESSONS.md from lessons.jsonl via render_lessons.py (never hand-merge the rendered file, AGENTS.md Rule 5). Sanitize any leaked workstation paths using relative paths or env vars (not username placeholders), or pre-commit repo_hygiene (LINT-006) blocks the merge commit. Use a throwaway git worktree to resolve without disturbing main's working tree.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_ad245568f6b1 -->
- Never put literal workstation paths ($HOME/...) in any tracked file — docs, scripts, memory, or config. Use relative paths (../../, ../filename), env vars ($OPENCLAW_ROOT, $REPO_ROOT), or ~ tilde. LINT-006 in repo_hygiene.py and CIDF Target Verification both enforce this as a pre-commit hard gate. The same rule applies to AI-generated memory content: use relative paths or strip the parent path tree; do NOT use <user> placeholder (it still exposes the directory structure).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_e4ee87395bd6 -->
- Hardware model affinity: Mac ALWAYS uses qwen3.5-9b-mlx (MLX); Windows ALWAYS uses qwen3.5-27b-claude-4.6-opus-reasoning-distilled-v2 (GGUF). NEVER cross-list MLX models for Windows or list Windows-only 27B/26B models for Mac.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_0baf339c8840 -->
- LM Studio reasoning models (Windows 27B) need max_tokens >= 4096 and commandTimeout >= 300s. They return reasoning_content not text; agent parsers must check reasoning_content as fallback.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_b07e175f1f09 -->
- Never force-push to main/master/shared branches without explicit human instruction naming the exact branch and the words 'force-push' or 'rewrite history'. After any suspected rewrite, run tree-twin scan (scripts/git/reanchor_scan.sh) before any git operation.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_a9bcfb96bafc -->
- Never hardcode workstation absolute paths in tracked files. Use ${HOME} or repo-relative paths (git rev-parse --show-toplevel) instead.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_d3ea8cb7aa83 -->
- All tracked text files must be UTF-8 without BOM on Windows. Always pass encoding='utf-8' to open(); set PYTHONUTF8=1 and PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8 in cross-platform scripts.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_0cbedad69b02 -->
- gbrain per-source sync must run from INSIDE that source's own git repo (its local_path). Use 'gbrain sync --repo <path> --source <id>' from a non-git cwd or neutral directory. A bare 'gbrain sync' from non-git cwd fails.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_f3f27933edc4 -->
- HITL rule: take explicit names verbatim; read the area's AGENTS.md before writing; if something seems wrong or you haven't read its conventions, STOP and ASK. NEVER override an explicit user instruction with a guess.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_3728e4df3666 -->
- Canonical skills stay in-repo. Local installs (.claude/skills, ~/.codex/skills, Hermes local skills) are thin wrappers only — trigger text + canonical repo root + redirect. Never copy canonical bodies, references, scripts, or assets into local installs.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_6a499cb69826 -->
- Nodes_modules/, __pycache__/, .venv/, dist/, build/ are never tracked. If git status hangs, check for committed node_modules.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ec398fedea0d -->
- Three-repo lockstep architecture: AlphaClaw (L1 infra) -> Perpetua-Tools (L2 runtime/state) -> orama-system (L3 planning/stateless). PT owns runtime/state; orama owns methodology/plans; AlphaClaw owns infrastructure. No shared contract change in one repo without matching change in the other, in the same session.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_1fa8fef1cdc3 -->
- Gate ladder: milestone-based migration gates 0-4. Gate 0 = foundations, Gate 1 = adapter working, Gate 2 = MCP+local agents operational, Gate 3 = orama first flow, Gate 4 = RC release. Do not skip gates; verify each before proceeding.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_b5c716f28c44 -->
- AFRP Intent-Verification anti-handwaving: never assert 'done/fine/nothing needed' from a narrow proxy. If a proxy disagrees with user insistence, run the real method exhaustively. Three STOP-and-clarify triggers: (1) interpretation risk, (2) about to conclude nothing-to-do, (3) explicit instruction vs my guess (catastrophic assumption). ASK first.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_a5213460aeb5 -->
- CIDF Target Verification: before inserting content anywhere, verify the destination (not just the method). (1) explicit user-given target is authoritative — use verbatim, (2) read the area's AGENTS.md/_index/README before writing, (3) verify not on a stale branch by comparing HEAD tree to origin.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ded5b47fb8b4 -->
- LINT-006: no absolute workstation paths in tracked files — use repo-relative paths or GitHub URLs. LINT-007: UTF-8 mojibake — pass encoding=utf-8 to open() on Windows. LINT-008: Co-author attribution — single source of truth in orama/scripts/git/check_commit_message.sh; sync outward; never hand-edit downstream copies.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_d543fb0347b6 -->
- gbrain source lifecycle: complete archival/removal in the SAME pass you decide it — never leave 'pending removal'. Old-path duplicate sources from repo moves resurface as sync_freshness/multi_source_drift every session. Use gbrain sources archive (reversible) + export defs to ~/repo-backups first. Autopilot launchd KeepAlive=true needs launchctl unload -w; kill won't stick.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_f5667a33c2f4 -->
- Strangler-fig migration pattern: for long-term migration, use HTTP-only adapter (never import internal modules). For one-time OS setup, apply patches to the installed binary. These are complementary, not competing. PT adapter calls patched binary via CLI/HTTP; orama applies OS-level patches once at install time.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_dd15b50b7f7d -->
- Companion-copy-until-verified migration pattern: files stay in source until proven in target — (1) copied to target, (2) target tests pass with copied version, (3) source tests still green. Do NOT delete from source until verified.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_b0776c20122f -->
- Worker primitive contract: workers are one generic primitive. TaskEnvelope(role, specialization, intent, prompt, depth=0) in, WorkerResult(status, summary, artifacts, errors) out. depth ALWAYS 0 in V1 (no sub-workers) — enforced by field_validator at runtime, not convention. Write artifacts to disk before returning status=done.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_3086671b02f6 -->
- Fail-closed at gateways: hardware affinity failures, missing PERPETUATOOLSROOT + explicit provider, unresolvable model IDs produce explicit errors — never silent fallback, never fail-open. Discovery tools (discover.py, network watcher) are exempt and must degrade gracefully.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_b5d28f5d6e08 -->
- Multi-agent scope claims: read docs/LESSONS.md first; append [IN PROGRESS: agent-name — file.py] before touching files; remove when done. Commit messages are the only async channel between agents sharing no session context — name changed constants/APIs.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_62f190f8c959 -->
- Version registry: single source of truth src/orama_system/_version.py __version__. Bump: edit _version.py only, then scripts/sync_version.py (25+ surfaces), then pytest tests/test_version_docs.py, then commit. Never manual-edit versioned surfaces.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_3a03dfe7c3bf -->
- macOS keep-both duplicates: Finder creates ' 2'/' 3' variants on collision. Check: find . -name '* 2.*' -o -name '* 3.*' | grep -v node_modules | grep -v .git; expect empty. Resolution: diff pairs, rename canonical from numbered copy, delete numbered. NEVER use Finder for tracked files — use git mv / cp+git rm.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_47072d788407 -->
- git internals macOS duplicates: .git/index 2/3 and .git/refs/heads/* 2 corrupt git state (repack fatals, wrong ref targets). Fix: rm numbered refs; git remote prune origin clears remote-tracking dupes. Check: find .git -name '* 2' -o -name '* 3'. .git should never be in iCloud-synced folder.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_1deda5c64215 -->
- Mask vs Fix principle: mask makes symptom disappear without removing root condition; fix removes condition so it cannot recur. Acceptable masks: time-boxed workaround for confirmed upstream bug. Unacceptable: resource leaks, concurrency bugs, security behavior. PR #69 singleFork was a mask — rejected upstream, removed in Vitest 4.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_10116c8a2fd5 -->
- Test flakiness taxonomy: (1) Timing races — pass alone, fail parallel → use await/async teardown/mock timers. (2) State leaks — pass fixed order, fail shuffled → beforeEach/afterEach reset. (3) Resource contention — EBUSY/SQLITE_BUSY/EADDRINUSE → close handles afterEach, unique temp paths, dynamic ports. (4) Environment differences — pass local, fail CI → pin versions, compare env specs. Decision tree: isolate → shuffle → check shared resources → compare environments.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_9e41693a15ec -->
- SQLite WAL contention fix: close DatabaseSync handles in afterEach; export close()/reset() from singleton modules. Use :memory: databases in tests. Never module-level singleton without teardown. Symptom: 5000ms vitest timeout cascades to 60s+ under parallel workers. Vitest 4 removed singleFork option.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_56d411b663a3 -->
- Upstream PR protocol: before submitting flake-fix PR, search existing issues/PRs/commits on upstream main (gh issue list --search, gh pr list --search, git log --oneline upstream/main). Submitting fix the maintainer already has in-flight wastes review time; submitting worse fix creates cleanup. PR #69 rejection: correct fix already on main.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_1e27c42719f8 -->
- ~~Tri-repo D1-D5 locked decisions: D1 versioning (PT 0.9.9.9, AlphaClaw MCP 0.9.16.9). D2 config: two distinct OpenClaw runtime agents vs orama methodology agents; PT bootstrap writes openclaw.json (D2-A), orama apply_runtime_payload (D2-B). D3 AlphaClaw feature/MacOS-post-install @ b540eca1 with lib/mcp+lib/agents present. D4 smoke auth precedence 1>2>3>4, fail-closed NO (non-interactive may use default password). D5 both entrypoints documented (CLI + FastAPI supervisor HTTP). Do not re-debate without human override.~~  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_292b1558dde2 superseded_by=lesson_9bcca08eefa4 -->
- Concurrent write coordination gap (v2.0): three repos have no agent write coordination — no branch-ownership registry, no per-repo/branch locks, no who-works-where state. Fix direction: worktree-per-agent enforced, PT-owned branch-ownership registry, per-repo/branch write locks with stale-takeover, pre-push gate rejecting other agents WIP commits. Human+agents are scheduled participants.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_140efb33eb17 -->
- Clean-lineage git hygiene for recovery branches: (1) dated branch names yyyy-mm-dd-001-brief-summary, (2) verify identity via scripts/git/check_identity.sh, (3) stash untracked before branch surgery, (4) manual-port reviewed intent into clean commits, (5) keep .env/.env.local/.paths untracked; update example files instead, (6) run python scripts/review/repo_hygiene.py . before opening PR.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_3ee30dd54440 -->
- Orphan branch recovery: symptoms = git merge-base HEAD origin/main exit 1; git rebase origin/main produces add/add conflicts on every file. Fix: git fetch origin main && git reset --hard origin/main; manually re-apply ~5 changed files. Prevention: always create feature branches from origin/main.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_f42cfecc56ba -->
- Multi-agent pre-commit checklist: (1) git fetch origin main && git log --oneline HEAD..origin/main; (2) grep -rn '192.168.' excluding tests/.env/LESSONS; (3) python -m pytest -q. Run before opening PR.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_f18c53cf68d3 -->
- Skill rename/migration tracking: when a skill is renamed (no-sleep-chains -> shell-hygiene), the old path becomes a redirect stub. The canonical target is bin/orama-system/skills/shell-hygiene/SKILL.md. Old cross-references in sibling repos (e.g., AlphaClaw/docs/Lessons.MD) become stale and must be updated. A redirect stub must never be edited for content.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_8b91c3032d29 -->
- Shell hygiene — sleep chains: sleep N && <command> and sleep N; <command> are blocked at the shell level. No workaround via shorter sleep chains (sleep 5 && sleep 5 && cmd also blocked). Enforcement is pre-execution; there is no escape hatch. Origin: 2026-05-16 npm install wait; re-triggered 2026-05-29 claude update wait.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ac92c47fd2b0 -->
- Shell hygiene — correct wait patterns: (1) run_in_background: true tasks — receive notification, then read output; no sleep needed. (2) File condition polling — until grep -q 'done|error|added|Updated|failed' file; do sleep 3; done. (3) File size polling — until [  -gt N ]; do sleep 2; done. (4) PID wait — until ! kill -0 PID; do sleep 2; done. (5) Port/service up — until curl -s http://host:port/health >/dev/null; do sleep 2; done.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_5e4490bd8eb8 -->
- Shell hygiene — zsh word splitting: zsh does NOT word-split unquoted  (SH_WORD_SPLIT off). WRONG: for id in  (fires once with all IDs joined); cmd  (all files as one arg). CORRECT: printf '%s\n' "" | while IFS= read -r id; do resolve ""; done; files=(a.py b.py); cmd "". Always quote expansions; these forms work identically in bash and zsh.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_20499ae79d37 -->
- Skill rename/migration pattern: when a skill is renamed (no-sleep-chains -> shell-hygiene, 2026-06-13), the old path becomes a redirect stub that must never be edited for content. Cross-references in sibling repos (AlphaClaw/docs/Lessons.MD) become stale and must be updated to the new canonical path. The canonical target is bin/orama-system/skill/<new-name>/SKILL.md relative to repo root.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_5bf0e69248ee -->
- reanchor_scan.sh canonical usage: reanchor_scan.sh <repo_path> [main_ref] [scope] where scope=remotes|heads|all. Builds tree-twin index from origin/main (%T format), walks first-parent history of each ref, finds deepest twin. Output states: MERGED/in-main (tip twin), NEEDS-REANCHOR (graft N commits onto twin), NO-TWIN (shares mb, likely fine), ORPHAN (mb==root). Verifies with: git cherry -v main_ref tip twin_base. This is the ONLY correct branch-state detector after a history rewrite.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_3189ca9025d1 -->
- sync_version.py canonical pattern: single source of truth src/orama_system/_version.py __version__. Bump workflow: edit _version.py only, then python3 scripts/sync_version.py (propagates to 25+ surfaces), then pytest tests/test_version_docs.py, then git commit. Dry-run: --dry-run; CI gate: --check (exits 1 if stale). Surfaces include SKILL.md frontmatter, CLAUDE.md, README badge, agent_registry.json, routing_rules.json, portal_server.py, install.ps1, PERPLEXITY_BRIDGE.md, SYNC_ANALYSIS.md, afrp/README.md, self-discovery/SKILL.md, bin/agents/*/agent.md, bin/mcp_servers/*.py, bin/shared/*.py. Historical docs (CHANGELOG, LESSONS, plans) intentionally excluded.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_bdda8ba253a0 -->
- ~~Git identity policy (2026-05-25): primary authors = <private email identity>, <private email identity>, Codex <codex@openai.com>. Co-authored-by allowed domains: openai.com, anthropic.com, cursor.com, cursor.sh, google.com, github.com, microsoft.com, azure.com, google.dev, perplexity.ai, x.ai, coderabbit.ai, mistral.ai, deepseek.com, cohere.com, meta.com, sourcegraph.com, devin.ai, codeium.com. Allowed Gmail co-authors: <private email identity>, <private email identity> only. Rejected: any other @gmail.com (unattributable). Install hooks per clone: bash scripts/git/install-local-hooks.sh. Check: bash scripts/git/check_identity.sh + bash scripts/git/check_commit_message.sh.~~  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_a45acb288e08 superseded_by=lesson_9f72c7949e38 -->
- Stash-first discipline: before any risky git operation (rebase, history inspection, branch surgery, cross-repo sync), capture state including untracked: git status --short --branch; git stash push --include-untracked -m 'preserve work before <operation>'; git rev-parse --is-shallow-repository; git config user.name; git config user.email. Never destructive cleanup until stash verified.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_993867a07023 -->
- AlphaClaw session startup sequence: (0) find . -name '* 2.*' -o -name '* 3.*' expect empty. (1) sync main from upstream (git fetch upstream main && git merge --ff-only). (2) sync pr-4-macos (git rebase origin/pr-4-macos && git merge --ff-only main). (3) sync feature/MacOS-post-install (git rebase origin/feature && git rebase pr-4-macos). (4) npm install && npm test on pr-4-macos. rebase --autostash required when rebasing over merge commits. End-of-session: commit docs/ to feature/MacOS-post-install, cherry-pick docs-only commits from agent branches.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_9fffe4530a95 -->
- Windows dev environment (lab machine): Node/npm anchored to LM Studio bin (REPO_ROOT\.lmstudio\.internal\utils\node.exe, REPO_ROOT\.lmstudio\tools\npm, shims in REPO_ROOT\.lmstudio\bin). git resolved dynamically from GitHub Desktop (do not hardcode versioned path). Never symlink dev tools into versioned app install folders on Windows. Verify: git --version; node --version; npm --version.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_c32fbd5580f8 -->
- Contaminated history expunge (last resort): tag pre-scrub backup, identify contaminated SHAs, cherry-pick good commits onto clean branch OR git filter-repo, force-with-lease push, delete contaminated remote branches (gh api -X DELETE), git remote prune origin, delete local refs at bad SHAs, git reflog expire --expire=now --all, git repack -Ad --unpack-unreachable=now, git prune --expire=now, verify messages + blobs + reflog drained. Notify collaborators to fresh clone. Rotate any secret that ever touched public remote.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_596c2ed27af1 -->
- Tree-twin re-anchor (correct fix after rewrite): build twin index (git log main --format='%H %T'), find deepest ancestor with same %T. Case A (exact tip twin): push --force-with-lease=old:old origin twin:branch. Case B (no tip twin): git rebase --onto twin base. Verify merge-base HEAD origin/main == twin SHA. Never flatten branches to main; re-anchor moves ref only, does not merge content.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_a8dafe878e84 -->
- Windows PowerShell runtime bootstrap (RTX/LM Studio host): prepend PATH with ~/.lmstudio/bin (shims), GitHub Desktop git mingw64/bin + cmd, set GIT_EXEC_PATH=mingw64/bin, npm_config_prefix=~/.lmstudio, use explicit .venv Python. Need git-remote-https.exe in mingw64/bin; if missing bash.exe, copy sh.exe to temp bash shim. jq may need separate install. Quote @{u} as git rev-parse --abbrev-ref '@{u}'; avoid && in older PowerShell.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_0d7986bfba36 -->

Final main() runs all checks, prints status, returns 1 on error.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_137adb15c1a6 -->

- When a content checker flags a file your grep says is clean, replicate the checker's EXACT matching before concluding false-positive — it may lowercase (case-insensitive). Also: content guards scan COMMENTS too, so stale config comments with banned IDs trip them; keep config comments current.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ec11f1ff55c0 -->

- ~~Never put literal workstation paths ($HOME/...) in any tracked file — docs, scripts, memory, or config. Use relative paths (../../, ../filename), env vars ($OPENCLAW_ROOT, $REPO_ROOT), or ~ tilde. LINT-006 in repo_hygiene.py and CIDF Target Verification both enforce this as a pre-commit hard gate. The same rule applies to AI-generated memory content: use relative paths or strip the parent path tree; do NOT use <user> placeholder (it still exposes the directory structure).~~  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_da04cbbae68b superseded_by=lesson_43943112eeed -->

The gmail_allowed() gate in check_commit_message.sh only runs when the email ends in @gmail.com or @googlemail.com. Any personal domain (e.g. a custom vanity domain) stored in ALLOWED_GMAIL_COAUTHORS will silently fall through to DENIED. Fix: put non-gmail personal/org addresses in ALLOWED_EXACT_COAUTHOR_EMAILS instead.~~  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_409c00dc67ee superseded_by=lesson_24a5a9ad9c76 -->

Never use username-containing placeholders or env vars that still expose directory structure (workspace-tree env-var path). The filename alone is sufficient when marked local-only; strip the parent path tree entirely. Standard: relative paths from the file's directory, or ~ for home, or $OPENCLAW_ROOT for the repo root. - datetime.fromtimestamp() returns naive local time — always pass tz=datetime.timezone.utc for UTC-aware output. Subtracting a naive datetime from an aware one raises TypeError. Also: datetime.now() without tz= is naive; use datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc) everywhere timestamps are compared or stored.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_3dc9dd3b6986 -->
- When updating lesson claims in lessons.jsonl, also update the matching sentinel-below bullet in LESSONS.md. Otherwise migrate_legacy_bullets re-reads the old bullet text, finds it absent from existing_claims (since we changed the claim), and re-appends it with the same hash-based ID. _dedupe_by_id keeps the LAST entry per ID, so the re-migrated OLD text wins. Fix: (1) update sentinel bullets to match new claim text, (2) dedup lessons.jsonl keeping FIRST occurrence.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_7a9bd3982e6f -->
- CodeRabbit reviews every new push to a PR, creating new unresolved threads even after all previous ones were resolved. Always check review thread state after each push, not just once at the start. Merge is only safe when BOTH conditions are true: (a) mergeStateStatus is CLEAN or MERGEABLE, and (b) all review threads are resolved.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_c057cdc0f899 -->
- Filesystem paths from compressed memory are not authoritative  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.405 evidence=1 id=lesson_7b4fcefd8537 -->
- Use reanchor_scan / cherry for branch parity on rewritten main  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.41 evidence=1 id=lesson_1a49775808a8 -->
- Push rejected: recover by fresh-from-main branch plus cherry-pick, never merge  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.49 evidence=1 id=lesson_859e6589c55f -->
- Filesystem path is authoritative; pinned path beats compressed memory  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.445 evidence=1 id=lesson_884b737ba7d1 -->
- Skill absorption: hermes-harness should absorb hermes-agent and pt-orama-harness-integration; perpetua-hardware should absorb local-inference  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_cd13c287bd02 -->
- Filesystem path is authoritative; pinned canonical path beats compressed recall  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.495 evidence=1 id=lesson_110bcd19d96a -->
- Canonical filesystem path must be treated as immutable source of truth across memory, writes, and recalls  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ef98574d9256 -->
- When a git pre-push hook runs from a deleted or removed worktree directory (getcwd fails), the hook exits non-zero with 'banned or unapproved attribution in commits to push' — even when NO actual banned pattern exists. The banned_attribution_lib.sh cannot resolve the repo root or patterns file, so it fails closed. The error message is misleading: the real cause is a dead working directory, not a real attribution violation. Fix: never delete a worktree before the git push completes; if a push fails with this message after a worktree cleanup, re-push from a live worktree or the canonical repo directory.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_1bb7057c6ea8 -->
- **Perpetua-Tools** = repo root (resolve via `$REPO_ROOT`; legacy absolute path sanitized)  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_4549b0ea8d50 -->
- Canonical base dir spelling is **`uLtrathink`**, with an uppercase `L`. Treat it as verbatim repo/test anchor. Do not normalize, lowercase, or "fix" it.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_723580aae34d -->
- Unified memory-trail hygiene rule: never write absolute workstation paths into tracked files. Resolve paths relative to the file's repo location (../../) or use repo-anchor env vars ($REPO_ROOT / $OPENCLAW_ROOT). Treat username placeholders (`<user>`) as still-doxxing because they leak directory structure. If a path can be made relative, it must be relative.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_ab002d123a7e -->

- git conflict resolution regex must close all open syntax constructs (arrays, objects) before emitting structural tokens like '} else {'. When extracting HEAD content from a conflict block that ends mid-array (e.g. plistContent = [ ... last-item, with no ].join), the merge output will have a dangling unclosed array. The fix: always verify node --check on the merged branch, not just the local branch. For if/else platform forks, ensure the darwin branch completes the array, closes the try block, and closes the if before the '} else {' for the Linux branch.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_9851f3606c7d -->
- When test expects 'must be a non-empty string' but source emits 'must be a string, got <type>', the root cause is an early guard raising before the canonical check. Refactoring: remove the type-specific early guard and let the single unified check ('not isinstance(val, str) or not val') cover both type mismatches and empty values. This produces one canonical error message that both the test and the error message agree on.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_d0ecd37438b4 -->
- AlphaClaw reverse-merge flow: feature/MacOS-post-install and pr-4-macos NEVER merge into main. The flow is: (1) merge open PRs into main, (2) sync from upstream chrysb/alphaclaw into main, (3) reverse-merge main INTO feature/MacOS-post-install. All merges use union strategy — never delete, always harmonize. Post-merge: always run 'node --check bin/alphaclaw.js' on the REMOTE branch (git show origin/feature/MacOS-post-install:bin/alphaclaw.js | node --check -), not just the local file.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_881de77084d5 -->
- Core lesson of the gbrain self-heal journey; applies beyond gbrain.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.535 evidence=2 id=lesson_60d836556ff0 -->

- When a parsed Co-authored-by email is present but not on the allowlist, attribution guards must reject immediately — display-name tool markers must never override a rejected email.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ddd40529a211 -->

- Windows dev environment: Node/npm anchored to LM Studio bin (`%USERPROFILE%\.lmstudio\.internal\utils\node.exe`, `%USERPROFILE%\.lmstudio\tools\npm`, shims in `%USERPROFILE%\.lmstudio\bin`). git resolved dynamically from GitHub Desktop (do not hardcode versioned path). Never symlink dev tools into versioned app install folders on Windows. Verify: git --version; node --version; npm --version.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_fbee16fcb67f -->
- After AlphaClaw macOS/cron merges, run node --check on large route test files before trusting Vitest parse errors — unclosed it() blocks surface as vite:import-analysis failures on line 1 const fs = require.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_6bff2fffe56e -->
- bin/alphaclaw.js must import git PATH helpers from lib/cli/git-runtime.js, not git-sync.js — git-sync only exports normalizeGitSyncFilePath and validateGitSyncFilePath; undefined prependGitShimDirToPath fails silently inside git shim try/catch.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_53c4e49a41d8 -->
- Per MIGRATION.md tri-repo contract: AlphaClaw L1 CI/runtime fixes stay in AlphaClaw until Gate 2 retirement of copied lib/mcp and lib/agents — PT does not duplicate L1 infrastructure patches; PT owns adapter HTTP+CLI lifecycle only.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_75dae01bcd29 -->
- REVIEW_QUEUE.md and other tracked memory summaries must sanitize workstation paths at render time via sanitize_tracked_path_leaks() — never echo raw candidate claim text containing /Users/ or OS-specific home-directory path form prefixes; fixing the queue file alone is a symptom patch.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_6fc89e22e3bb -->
- Windows .cmd/.bat files require CRLF line endings. cmd.exe tokenises on CRLF; LF-only batch files silently fail or produce garbled output. Fix in Python: open in binary mode and join lines with '\r\n'. Verify: xxd my.cmd | grep -c '0d 0a' must equal line count. Git prevention: declare '*.cmd text eol=crlf' in .gitattributes. Root cause confirmed in orama-system PR #108 (gstack-brain-sync.cmd). Documented in docs/wiki/08-git-hygiene-and-branching.md.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_195f34935121 -->
- cursor-agent vs agent disambiguation: cursor-agent (~/.local/bin/cursor-agent) is Cursor's native background agent CLI; agent (~/.grok/bin/agent) is the Grok Build TUI. Always invoke Cursor agents as cursor-agent. Model for light-task fanout: claude-4.6-sonnet-medium (Sonnet 4.6 Medium). Pattern: cursor-agent --print --model claude-4.6-sonnet-medium 'task'. Skill: orama-system/bin/orama-system/skills/cursor-agent/SKILL.md  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_337083e3a723 -->
- Platform affinity bias: Mac/Linux installations prefer AlphaClaw + OpenClaw (start.sh). Windows installations prefer Hermes Harness (start.ps1). ECC (vendor/ecc-tools) bridges both — orama-system skills run unmodified in both harnesses in v1 and v2/oramasys. Canonical routing doc: orama-system/bin/orama-system/skills/hermes-harness/references/platform-affinity-routing.md  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_67644b17d119 -->
- cursor-agent model defaults: composer-2.5 is the default model for light tasks; auto is the fallback when composer-2.5 unavailable. claude-4.6-sonnet-medium is an orchestrator-override only — used exclusively when Opus 4.8 Ultracode or Fable 5 workflow explicitly demands it for a fan-out subtask. Skill: orama-system/bin/orama-system/skills/cursor-agent/SKILL.md  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_cdb4a69ba920 -->
- All .agent/memory writers must call sanitize_tracked_path_leaks() from path_hygiene.py before persisting strings — episodic log_execution, learn.py, graduate.py, and review queue renderer. Legacy rows: run scrub_memory_paths.py then re-render LESSONS.md; never hand-edit derived markdown to hide paths.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_3e554559c7ed -->
- discover.py must split hash endpoints (LAN IP) from runtime endpoints (localhost on Windows) — never mutate endpoints win to LAN IP before patch_openclaw_json or lmstudio-win breaks  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_0314ada4d630 -->

- routing.json consumed by alphaclaw_bootstrap must pass jsonschema validation and RFC-1918 hostname allowlist before build_openclaw_config — never trust filesystem JSON without _validate_pt_state (T1-A).  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_e45608de48c0 -->

Denylist check_no_hallucinated_models.py is insufficient alone (S7).  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_34bb51037fce -->

- macOS ships bash 3.2 as /bin/bash — it lacks mapfile/readarray. scripts/git/*.sh hook scripts must use while IFS= read -r loops. Symptom: check_tdd_commit.sh exit 127 mapfile: command not found. Canonical ref: orama git-history-surgery/references/bash-32-git-script-portability.md.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.65 evidence=1 id=lesson_f8e2a91b4c3d -->
- oramaclaw pending conflicts cleared or orphaned by dead transactions must be archived to state_dir/registry/orphan-conflicts/ before removal — never silent delete (T3-C). sweep_orphan_pending runs on ControlStore.open.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_0a52069a7fa0 -->
- Block agentic-stack upstream Brain integration in .agent until brain_bridge.py supports both Gbrain and codejunkie99/brain — Gbrain via gstack is canonical for PT-orama RAG  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_144d7beb4018 -->
- ~/.gbrain/config.json must have a 'database_url' key explicitly — ~/.gbrain/.env is NOT auto-sourced in non-interactive shells (Claude Code agents, launchd background jobs). Export the URL from .env into config.json directly, or gbrain fails with 'database_url is missing from config' in every non-interactive context.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_163065104e2a -->
- SKILL.md YAML frontmatter must be the first bytes of the file — place lint-ignore HTML comments after the closing frontmatter delimiter  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_18dfc39b743f -->
- patch_models_yml must skip loopback win_ip the same way patch_devices_yml does — never persist localhost into shared YAML defaults  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_1bc5fdd8e247 -->
- PR descriptions and summaries are append-only historical records: never replace the original purpose with a follow-up job summary. When new work lands (CodeRabbit fixes, merge resolutions, CI notes), add a ## Follow-up section below the existing scope — same rule as LESSONS.md and integrative-merge doctrine.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_1f08ce9fa51a -->
- Pre-v2 security hardening (v1.1.1.0): all Linux-runnable tiers ship on cursor/security-hardening-pre-v2-c4ae in both repos; Mac/Win live E2E (start.sh, Ollama probes, LM_STUDIO_WIN_ENDPOINTS, hardware-policy harness, keychain) and T5 freeze tags stay blocked until tomorrow on real machines. Plan: orama-system/docs/plans/2026-06-27-security-hardening-pre-v2.md — platform schedule table marks each tier.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_27424f2d7fa7 -->
- When git pull is blocked by local commits, use git pull --rebase origin main. If the local commit content is already upstream (e.g. a duplicate episodic memory entry), rebase drops it silently with 'dropping...patch contents already upstream' — this is correct, not data loss.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_2856d7b3b7f3 -->
- Co-authored-by guards must fail-closed on parsed email: if angle-bracket email is present but not allowlisted, reject immediately — display-name markers (hermes, qwen, claude) never override. Fuzz cases in tests/test_git_attribution_guard.py (T3-B).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_2aa89200cecf -->
- Hermes thin wrappers: four required (council, review, delegate, hardware-policy); lesson-mining is optional via --include-optional only — orama-system has no Perpetua-Tools dependency for graduation  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_3d15c25af434 -->
- After a main history rewrite, never classify branches by merge-base exit 1 or ahead/behind counts alone — use scripts/git/reanchor_scan.sh plus git cherry -v; merge-base failure often means rewrite boundary not orphan work.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_41d3bfca818a -->
- Before destructive git ops (rebase all, branch delete, history surgery), append a markdown branch catalog to .agent/memory/working/BRANCH_CATALOG_COMPLETE_<date>.md using reanchor_scan output — read-only snapshot for triage.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_4fab986d5cfc -->
- Merge-conflict synthesize mode: when both branches changed the same region for valid different reasons, blend both — do not pick one side wholesale. Example PR #158: keep ipaddress-based _validate_endpoint_host from security-hardening AND locality/canonical-loopback tests from #157.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_512dcbfb94de -->
- v2 oramasys/agate repo will own model_hardware_policy.yml schema, hardware_policy API, and hardware_policy_cli — perpetua-core imports agate; PT v1 paths are migration source only  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_5ca2112b2a5a -->
- LINT-006 flags OS-specific home-directory path forms and $HOME patterns even inside policy docs that WARN against them — use placeholder notation abstract home-directory placeholders or OS-specific home-directory path in ALL docs explaining what not to do.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_5f5ab082e311 -->
- Mac—”Win cross-harness E2E verification requires an actual curl probe: curl -s --max-time 5 http://${WIN_IP}:1234/v1/models — the start.sh --hardware-policy flag only validates openclaw.json config, it does NOT probe the live Win LM Studio endpoint.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_6077e426bb8c -->
- routing.json consumed by alphaclaw_bootstrap must pass jsonschema validation and RFC-1918 hostname allowlist before build_openclaw_config — never trust filesystem JSON without _validate_pt_state (T1-A).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_60f9b192e2a0 -->
- setup_macos.py step_mac_agent_thinking must guard against string model shorthand — openclaw.json agents may use 'model': '<id>' (string) instead of {"primary": ...}. Guard with isinstance(_model, str): return before calling .get('primary'). Without guard: AttributeError crashes start.sh --status silently.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_61d63d446119 -->
- All .agent/memory writers must call sanitize_tracked_path_leaks() from path_hygiene.py before persisting strings — episodic log_execution, learn.py, graduate.py, and review queue renderer. Legacy rows: run scrub_memory_paths.py then re-render LESSONS.md; never hand-edit derived markdown to hide paths.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_700d472a2220 -->
- T5 tagging procedure: git tag v1.1.1 -m '<message>' then git push origin v1.1.1 in BOTH repos in the same session. No version bump needed if pyproject.toml/\_version.py already reflect the target version. Tag only after ALL E2E gates pass (Mac E2E + Win E2E + Mac—’Win cross-probe).  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_80a8b4101160 -->
- agentic-stack lives at vendor/agentic-stack in Perpetua-Tools like ecc-tools — orama start.sh symlinks lib/shared/agentic_stack from PT vendor path  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_8134a6cf1809 -->
- discover.py must split hash endpoints (LAN IP) from runtime endpoints (localhost on Windows) — never mutate endpoints win to LAN IP before patch_openclaw_json or lmstudio-win breaks  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_91f19059ee87 -->
- orama-system TDD gate (pre-commit check for adjacent *.test.ts(x) when web/src/ production files change) fires on merge resolution commits even when no new code was authored. Override with 'tdd-skip: <reason>' in the git commit message body — the commit-msg hook reads this line and bypasses the TDD requirement.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_a04a8dd3b911 -->
- Security and API fixes must trace the full data path from source to sink — a helper fix (e.g. _canonical_endpoint on return paths) does not protect import-time constants, module-level env reads, or unvalidated JSON consumers that bypass the helper.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_a19b581aee32 -->
- At Mac session start after a Win session, always read REVIEW_QUEUE.md FIRST — the Win session completes tasks that Mac thought were pending. Never repeat work the Win session already did.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_b194920de806 -->
- After vendor/agentic-stack submodule init run install-agentic-stack.sh then agentic-stack upgrade --dry-run before harmonizing PT .agent/ — union-merge only, never commit into vendor/  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_be2a10129cad -->
- When adding Windows .cmd shims in Python always write CRLF bytes and add gitattributes eol=crlf — LF-only batch files fail silently under cmd.exe per PR #108 gstack-brain-sync.cmd incident  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_cea128a023a9 -->
- Dream-cycle salience scoring must compare UTC-aware timestamps — episodic entries use +00:00 offsets; naive datetime.now() minus aware fromisoformat raises TypeError and blocks auto_dream.py.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_e3680054a078 -->
- xAI Grok Build (Elon Musk) installs a bare agent CLI at ~/.grok/bin/agent — it is NOT Cursor. PATH collision: scripts invoking agent without a path hit Grok TUI instead of cursor-agent. Always call cursor-agent explicitly and document both binaries in harness skills.  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_e9b8b2213874 -->
- setup_macos.py step_mac_agent_thinking must guard against string model shorthand — openclaw.json agents may use 'model': '<id>' (string) instead of {"primary": ...}. Guard with isinstance(_model, str): return before calling .get('primary'). Without guard: AttributeError crashes start.sh --status silently.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_10620fe7285b -->
- ~/.gbrain/config.json must have a 'database_url' key explicitly — ~/.gbrain/.env is NOT auto-sourced in non-interactive shells (Claude Code agents, launchd background jobs). Export the URL from .env into config.json directly, or gbrain fails with 'database_url is missing from config' in every non-interactive context.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_caabfb16dfa1 -->
- git add --renormalize with 'text eol=crlf' in .gitattributes can store raw CRLF bytes in the blob on macOS, creating a phantom git diff where cmp shows working-tree and HEAD blob are byte-identical yet git diff HEAD shows 69+ lines changed. Fix: write LF content to file, git add (normalizes to LF blob), commit locally, then git pull --rebase drops it as 'patch already upstream'.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_37ed595a3b57 -->
- orama-system TDD gate (pre-commit check for adjacent *.test.ts(x) when web/src/ production files change) fires on merge resolution commits even when no new code was authored. Override with 'tdd-skip: <reason>' in the git commit message body — the commit-msg hook reads this line and bypasses the TDD requirement.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_47847426bd94 -->
- PR landing in orama-system and Perpetua-Tools always uses squash-merge (gh pr merge --squash). When a PR shows CONFLICTING/DIRTY on GitHub: (1) git merge origin/main locally, (2) resolve conflicts additively (union both sides), (3) push branch, (4) gh pr merge --squash. Never regular merge commit; never rebase-merge. squash keeps main history linear.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_001158f408c1 -->
- SSRF validation must convert IPv4-mapped IPv6 addresses before checking is_link_local: if isinstance(addr, ipaddress.IPv6Address) and addr.ipv4_mapped is not None: addr = addr.ipv4_mapped. Without this, ::ffff:169.254.x.x passes ipv6.is_link_local=False check even though the underlying IPv4 address is link-local. Fixed in PT PR #154 alphaclaw_bootstrap.py.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_a88a10dfcba5 -->
- LAN peer WS+SSE state machine: WS_CONNECTING (5s) -> WS_CONNECTED | fail(x2) -> SSE_CONNECTING -> SSE_CONNECTED | DISCONNECTED (30s retry -> WS). Heartbeat every 15s. Two consecutive WS failures demote to SSE before retry cycle. Transport abstraction = only lan_peer_channel.py knows which wire is live.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.9 evidence=1 id=lesson_legacy_8670b3569ccd -->
- PT memory pipeline (promote.py, auto_dream.py, and siblings) must use open(path, encoding="utf-8") for every .md and .jsonl read on Windows. The default cp1252 codec silently works until LESSONS.md gains a non-ASCII character (em-dash, smart quote, arrow) — then crashes with UnicodeDecodeError on byte 0x9D. Fixed in PT 0ed5635. Audit all open() calls in .agent/memory/*.py.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.95 evidence=2 id=lesson_legacy_8a8f1dbdd852 -->
- LAN P2P bidirectional transport (2-host Python/FastAPI): use FastAPI WebSocket as primary (zero new deps, dual-socket, ~40 LoC) with SSE+POST as HTTP-only fallback (also zero deps, 2 connections). Channel manager (lan_peer_channel.py) abstracts both transports behind send()/on_inbound(). Shared JSON envelope {type, source, ts, data} makes transport invisible to callers.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.9 evidence=1 id=lesson_legacy_e0fb9321d79f -->
- macOS Keychain service names for OpenClaw: 'openclaw.gemini-main-apikey' (->OPENCLAW_MODELS_PROVIDERS_GEMINI_MAIN_APIKEY), 'openclaw.gemini-fallback-apikey' (->OPENCLAW_MODELS_PROVIDERS_GEMINI_FALLBACK_APIKEY), 'openclaw.telegram-bot-token' (->OPENCLAW_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN), 'openclaw.gateway-auth-token' (->OPENCLAW_GATEWAY_AUTH_TOKEN). Store: printf '%s' VALUE | bash $OPENCLAW_ROOT/orama-system/scripts/openclaw/store_keychain_secret.sh <service>. Load at startup: source $OPENCLAW_ROOT/orama-system/scripts/openclaw/load_keychain_secrets.sh  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_2a6766b489c5 -->
- Always serialize timestamps in UTC to avoid cross-region comparison bugs  <!-- status=legacy confidence=0.7 evidence=0 id=lesson_legacy_74d824bb84e2 -->
- Portal peer-file 404: pull orama 86c90bc+, restart --lan-peer; peer-stream needs response_class=StreamingResponse  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.9 evidence=1 id=lesson_legacy_peerstream_20260628 -->
- Post-commit sync: git fetch --prune, pull --rebase origin main, push, verify HEAD equals origin/main in both repos  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.9 evidence=1 id=lesson_legacy_sync_rebase_20260628 -->
- LM Studio loads only one model per instance; probe state=loaded via /api/v0/models before dispatch  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.59 evidence=1 id=lesson_712a5068a678 -->
- Windows partner CLIs use parametric LOCALAPPDATA and USERPROFILE paths; bootstrap with ensure-partner-cli-paths.ps1  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_5d96448dd529 -->
- Codex CLI v0.142.x uses three dispatch profiles: fanout (codex exec -C repo -s workspace-write --dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox), bounded (exec workspace-write only), interactive (--sandbox danger-full-access --ask-for-approval never). Legacy --approval-mode removed in 0.140+. Resolve repo root and Codex binary at runtime; never hardcode host paths in prompts.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_2cef6113c1f1 -->
- Hermes skill absorption: redirect stubs and .agents wrappers stay thin; canonical bodies live only in bin/orama-system/skills. perpetua-hardware in .agents targets hardware-affinity-gate (orama methodology); PT runtime SSoT stays in Perpetua-Tools policy YAML. Archive llm-council-orchestration is SUPERSEDED by pt-orama-council; Win LM Studio coder is qwen3.5-27b-claude-4.6-opus-reasoning-distilled-v2 not invented Qwen 3.6 Coder.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ed634fbe6cd0 -->
- Hermes thin wrappers: four required (council, review, delegate, hardware-policy); lesson-mining is optional via --include-optional only — orama-system has no Perpetua-Tools dependency for graduation  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_6ce521f323f4 -->
- Hermes dispatch envelope: L3 skill_id+args+agent_id; L2 adds orama_system_root, executor_id, optional transport partner profile; L0 OpenClaw core result plus optional Hermes extensions  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_f6de10a70a81 -->
- ~~LINT-006 flags OS-specific home-directory path forms and $HOME patterns even inside policy docs that WARN against them — use placeholder notation abstract home-directory placeholders or OS-specific home-directory path in ALL docs explaining what not to do.~~  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_8c6e3368a308 superseded_by=lesson_eb4a1cb51115 -->
- Mac↔Win cross-harness E2E verification requires an actual curl probe: curl -s --max-time 5 http://${WIN_IP}:1234/v1/models — the start.sh --hardware-policy flag only validates openclaw.json config, it does NOT probe the live Win LM Studio endpoint.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_bd8505031496 -->
- At Mac session start after a Win session, always read REVIEW_QUEUE.md FIRST — the Win session completes tasks that Mac thought were pending. Never repeat work the Win session already did.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_20586d86a285 -->
- When git pull is blocked by local commits, use git pull --rebase origin main. If the local commit content is already upstream (e.g. a duplicate episodic memory entry), rebase drops it silently with 'dropping...patch contents already upstream' — this is correct, not data loss.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_66d2f9a8432a -->
- T5 tagging procedure: git tag v1.1.1 -m '<message>' then git push origin v1.1.1 in BOTH repos in the same session. No version bump needed if pyproject.toml/\_version.py already reflect the target version. Tag only after ALL E2E gates pass (Mac E2E + Win E2E + Mac→Win cross-probe).  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_601a930aa10d -->
- When git commit output is piped through '| tail -N', a commit failure can look like success because the error message is truncated. Always check 'git status --short' after a commit that used tail-truncated output to confirm the file is no longer staged.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_18f5ca481f90 -->
- Mac and Win orama-system installs self-talk over LAN via HTTP only: peer IP from last_discovery.json, inference via LM Studio /v1/models, control-plane via portal /health and /api/status after PORTAL_BIND_LAN=1 and shared ORAMA_CONTROL_PLANE_TOKEN on both hosts — use probe_lan_peer.py or Hermes /lan-peer-self-talk; no SSH and no second RPC layer  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_12ddf8cf63b9 -->
- Windows start.ps1 must parse bash-style flags from args (--no-open --stop --status) because PowerShell switch params reject --kebab-case; never assign to dollar-pid — use listenerPid — PID is a read-only automatic variable  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_0d5d6b4a25eb -->
- Windows orama uvicorn launch must match start.sh: orama_system.api_server and portal_server modules with PYTHONPATH repo src and root — bare api_server colon app fails silently  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_52add7792e48 -->
- PowerShell ProcessStartInfo EnvironmentVariables is a StringDictionary — use ContainsKey not Contains or Start-Service throws before uvicorn launches  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_51f853af60eb -->
- After start.ps1 rehab on Windows verify PT 8000 orama 8001 Portal 8002 UP via start.ps1 --status before LAN peer probe  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_998b0a438dd4 -->
- Windows LAN peer self-talk succeeds when .env.local sets PORTAL_BIND_LAN=1 and ORAMA_BIND_LAN=1, start.ps1 is launched via powershell.exe -File with LF line endings, and last_discovery.json is current  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_2e8c4ea72453 -->
- NEVER run git clean -fd or git clean -fdx without backup or stash  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.425 evidence=1 id=lesson_d66d6afdaefb -->
- If the only goal is 'worktree matches remote', use git restore --staged --worktree . and manually remove confirmed files; do not destroy untracked files first  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_23e3d608b63e -->
- LAN peer co-orchestration uses markdown file drops via lan_peer_assign.py and POST /api/peer-file to ~/.openclaw/state/lan_peer/inbox/. Fan-out manifest JSON splits topics by assignee mac or win. WS and SSE are for heartbeat and probe only; large autoresearch payloads are plain files.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_87636d658879 -->
- lan_peer_assign.py resolves orama repo root as Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4], not parents[5]. Local inbox import requires orama_system on sys.path from repo/src. parents[5] pointed at OpenClaw parent and caused ModuleNotFoundError.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_cbcd146eb431 -->
- lan_peer_assign fanout continues on per-item peer drop failures and returns status partial with exit code 1 when any remote drop fails while local assignments succeed. Mac can proceed while Win catches up on git pull and portal restart.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_46944e3665db -->
- On Win, Mac-to-Win assignments arrive in the local lan_peer inbox. Win operators read inbound files with lan_peer_assign.py list and read --name without --peer. Inbound peer files are already local after Mac drop --peer.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_206dc2c869c3 -->
- Win co-orchestrator replies use lan_peer_assign.py drop --peer --file with assignee mac. Mac reads Win deliverables via list --peer and read --peer --name. Standard deliverables: gpu-results.md, win-code-review.md, win-self-improve-runtime-results.md.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_dc67a19abdf7 -->
- ~~autoresearch_bridge.py sync_autoresearch_idempotent uses SSH to GPU_BOX by default. LAN co-orchestration on Win uses HTTP LM Studio and file inbox instead. Local Win preflight must not block on SSH timeout when the operator runs on the Win host.~~  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_7791e7860857 superseded_by=lesson_aeb3cd01c203 -->
- Mac must git pull orama-system main at or after 9f89051 and restart with ./start.sh --lan-peer --no-open before /api/peer-file accepts Win drops. portal-health can PASS while peer-file returns 404 on an old portal process.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_130073d9e30a -->
- LAN peer joint auth mode accepts PT lane token from Perpetua-Tools .state/control_plane_token plus orama env lanes. probe_lan_peer.py reports auth_mode joint when both lanes are configured; either key unlocks portal-status on probes.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_43c39af8176f -->
- On Mac when Ollama is warm and LM Studio is passive-only, route Mac co-orchestrator and subagent inference through ollama-mac at localhost:11434. LM Studio :1234 may still answer peer-lmstudio probes with a model catalog but must not be the primary execution path unless hardware policy requires MLX on that port.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_49a5af119f6f -->
- Cross-repo model-endpoint-policy parity CI on pull_request must checkout the sibling repository at github.head_ref (same branch name), not main — stacked security PRs diverge from main until merge; rm -rf sibling dir before main fallback to avoid partial checkout.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_4b3d787319a1 -->
- Cross-repo Python policy parity scripts must compare function ASTs with ast.dump(), not ast.unparse() — quote-style and formatting differences cause false diverge failures even when logic is identical.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_c97cfd0679bb -->
- validate_model_endpoint_url must catch urlparse().port ValueError on malformed ports and re-raise ModelEndpointPolicyError — uncaught ValueError becomes HTTP 500 instead of policy rejection.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_816433497c31 -->
- Control plane token persistence must use _secure_write_token with os.open O_CREAT and mode 0600 — never plain open().write() for secrets on disk.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_67a7e561207f -->
- GET /health must validate MODEL_ENDPOINT_URL through validate_model_endpoint_url before any outbound probe — blocks SSRF when env injection supplies a non-loopback URL.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_1d3b8cae0eef -->
- POST /runtime/bootstrap logging must pass payloads through redact_runtime_payload before emission — never log raw bootstrap bodies that may contain tokens or secrets.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_fb42336ab528 -->
- Portal lifecycle mutating routes (stop, restart, rediscover) require same-origin Origin or Referer guards in addition to bearer token — CSRF protection is mandatory; session cookies are optional UX only per SECURITY.md.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_3e31f4040c55 -->
- Stacked cross-repo security PRs may intentionally share the same branch name (e.g. cursor/security-pr1-pr2-auth-hardening-f559) — parity CI head_ref checkout is what binds sibling repos, not branch-name coincidence alone.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_c935b0102fb9 -->
- Tests and docs must not embed $HOME/ or other workstation-specific absolute paths — repo_hygiene LINT-006 blocks them; use <local-temp> placeholders or repo-relative paths in fixtures.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_cc6cbd625069 -->
- Portal HTML rendering must escape all user-controlled strings with html.escape (or equivalent _tag helper) before embedding in templates — never interpolate raw query params or flash messages into HTML.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_94097ce6f710 -->
- After a PR merges, always verify origin/main contains every commit on the feature branch — unattended merges and continued pushes to the same branch name leave post-merge commits orphaned until a follow-up PR is opened.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_0425d3219be5 -->
- Do not assume a branch tip is merged just because its PR shows MERGED — GitHub merges the PR snapshot at merge time; agents that push after merge create a divergent branch tip that main never receives.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_005241c94fc6 -->
- When multiple AI agents share a branch name across repos or sessions, treat merge time as a hard boundary — scan git log origin/main..origin/<branch> on both repos before closing work, and open a fresh follow-up branch/PR for any commits landed after merge.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ff3b885b1eae -->
- When ws-peer probe PASS is bidirectional between Mac and Win portal, L3 file inbox co-orchestration is fully operational. Mac uses OpenClaw plus Ollama warm; Win uses Hermes plus LM Studio 27B. Coordinate only via lan_peer_assign file drops, not remote agent RPC.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_20833366511b -->
- Windows Portal http://localhost:8002/ Internal Server Error on dashboard root: pull orama-system main at or after 435d27a and restart start.ps1 --lan-peer. Fix unwraps redacted agents dict in portal_server _render_html; /health may PASS while / returns 500 on stale portal process.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_78f4700bd60c -->
- Portal / returned HTTP 500 after restart if portal_server is not updated first: rerun start.ps1 --lan-peer after pulling latest origin/main  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_64dedfe61cfa -->
- Rebase or cherry-pick post-merge orphan PRs onto current main before closing or merging: first-commit conflicts with main usually mean the work already landed via a different PR path.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_30de39cf9ada -->
- Draft PR triage: three-dot diff inflates post-merge orphan PRs; always compare two-dot at branch tip vs main and cherry-pick net-new commits instead of merging stale multi-hundred-commit-behind branches.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_5222ac71c9ef -->
- active_tilting Win Ollama models must resolve to port 11434 not lm-studio :1234: detect_active_tilting_ip returns the LM Studio endpoint; ModelRegistry._resolve_host must branch on backend=ollama and rebuild http://hostname:port from models.yml.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_50e33b4bf1ec -->
- Co-orchestration mutations use subagent branches named subagent/role/topic from origin/main; coordination stays on main via file inbox.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ee24710b4c2f -->
- Win LAN peer inbox canonical URL is /peer-inbox served from platform/windows/peer_inbox_portal.py. /co-orchestration/windows and /co-orchestration on Win hosts 307 redirect to /peer-inbox. Hermes co_orchestration_windows skin removed.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.65 evidence=1 id=lesson_0762f924239d -->
- When parallel Task subagents hit harness usage limits, parent agent executes the assignment inline on the same host and still drops lan_peer_assign deliverables to the peer inbox. Do not block co-orchestration on subagent quota.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_0ec02977f23a -->
- autoresearch_bridge preflight AUTORESEARCH_PREFLIGHT_MODE auto uses http-local when GPU_BOX host is loopback or local IP: local git sync in LOCAL_REPO_PATH and HTTP GET LM Studio /v1/models. - PowerShell ops scripts under orama-system must use ASCII-only punctuation in string literals; Unicode em-dash or smart quotes cause ParserError on Windows PowerShell 5.x and break background monitors like coord_monitor.ps1.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_c391481ca104 -->
- When Mac and Win both push Perpetua-Tools .agent/memory in the same coord round, git pull may conflict on lessons.jsonl and LESSONS.md; union-merge all lesson rows from both sides and never drop Mac learn.py rows or Win operator-approve rows.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_8b5d45070494 -->

- Pulse unified coord-019: both hosts use win_job_queue and mac_job_queue pulse-gate, last_pulse_seen inbox diff, BLOCKED_PENDING in Python only; Win keeps role-specific agent cards and coord_monitor listen; Mac keeps flock lock and job_cycle_listen reset-on-job.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ea4571839e9f -->
- P5 T1 sign_operator_payload embeds expires_at inside HMAC canonical JSON; verify uses secrets.compare_digest and rejects expired tokens.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_b17b09dbf7c0 -->
- Lock P5 decisions in docs/plans/P5-DECISIONS-LOCKED.md on implementation branch; amend via explicit PR note not silent drift.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ea2950bef831 -->
- P5 implementation ships on cursor/security-pr3-swarm-approval-f559; main stays at planning until PR merge unblocks L1 BLOCKED_PENDING and l1_dispatch API routes.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_c0caf60f0cb8 -->
- /autoplan on execution plan produced GSTACK REVIEW REPORT with amendments A1-A6 before coding; lock decisions in P5-DECISIONS-LOCKED.md to prevent silent drift.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_46c828cb56e6 -->
- After T1 merge on branch, l1_dispatch _p5_landed import succeeds but CLI still blocked until portal /api/l1/* wired in T2-T7.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_fa0c5c1f8caa -->
- PT lessons.jsonl parallel Mac Win pushes cause rebase conflicts; union-merge jsonl lines split concatenated entries then render_lessons.py before rebase --continue.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_8e3f51cc9de9 -->
- P5 closes client approved bool not human review: HMAC proves preview endpoint ran; stolen bearer can still auto preview-launch within TTL.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_44b19fa16e6b -->
- Win queue idle after job burst is expected; synthesize win-coder cards from Mac inbox intel when orchestrator has backlog.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_6a40e7cdcc09 -->
- Post-ship rinse: learn all interaction lessons, auto_dream x3, push both repos, peer drop artifacts, coord_pulse dry-run x3, coord_monitor 15m listen.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_a5d22ead6088 -->
- P5 T2 next: api_swarm_preview returns preview_id approval_token expires_at signed via sign_operator_payload with assignments_hash in canonical payload.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_aa77d4508b52 -->
- P5 T2 api_swarm_preview returns preview_id approval_token expires_at via _sign_swarm_preview and assignments_hash; preview tests need bearer header when auth enforced.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_f3147e01a67d -->
- job_cycle_listen.log and coord_monitor ticks are coord telemetry (sync/probe/gate), not GossipBus mesh transport; v2 gossip uses HTTP tail/ingest of redacted GossipBus events; file inbox stays audit trail  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_eecb0f71cc4f -->
- Anti-doxxing must be wired at every layer: protocols/path-hygiene.md, AGENTS.md, permissions.md never-rule, path_hygiene.py sanitize, Cursor alwaysApply rules in PT and orama, and repo_hygiene CI — not just LESSONS bullets  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_37e04950f3fe -->
- sanitize_tracked_path_leaks must redact workspace tree paths (Downloads/SKILLS.md/ultrathink variants) to <workspace-root> after home-prefix substitution — %USERPROFILE% alone is not sufficient for tracked memory  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_992cafaca36b -->
- Reject review-queue candidates that promote personal Downloads or USERPROFILE workspace trees as canonical; use orama-system + Perpetua-Tools repo names and REPO_ROOT env anchors instead  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_324689a515a7 -->
- ~~Policy docs that explain forbidden path patterns must use placeholder notation OS-specific home-directory paths or OS-specific home-directory path form — never literal OS-specific home-directory segments or the linter will fail even on warning docs~~  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_889a1c180031 superseded_by=lesson_460ac276e494 -->
- 15-minute coord_monitor with Mac peer reachable and win queue 0 pending / added=[] is healthy idle — not a failure; next work is feature-branch P5 T3 or Mac-orchestrated fan-out  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_3a690b020b00 -->
- P5 swarm HITL on cursor/security-pr3-swarm-approval-f559: T1 operator payload + T2 preview signing done (2/7); T3 launch token verify is next; L1 /api/l1/* stays blocked until PR merges  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_4ad1b44cc918 -->
- PT lessons.jsonl rebase conflicts with Mac parallel pushes: union-merge rows by lesson id, fix concatenated candidate JSON, run render_lessons.py, then git rebase --continue — never hand-edit LESSONS.md  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_357eb41fde9b -->
- Operator triple-rinse after coord jobs: push both repos, learn.py batch graduate lessons, auto_dream.py x3, lan_peer_assign drop to Mac, coord_pulse x3, push again  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_a8bcd373c9bd -->
- coord-023 drained 18 ack queue items; triple rinse loop runs 3 outer cycles of pulse+learn+push then 3x15m listen; queue down to mac-orchestrator-self-improve-003 plus researcher backlog  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_83b6abe6f405 -->
- Mac portal at 192.168.254.102:8002 can flap (timeout on pulses 1-2) then recover on pulse 3 in the same batch — retry peer drops after portal-health PASS, do not treat transient timeout as Mac offline  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_5baad6942a2a -->
- win-coord-listen-022 assignment: Mac orchestrator requests Win run coord_monitor -Minutes 45 (three 15m ticks) in parallel with Mac job_cycle_listen; post ack win-coord-022-mac-gossip-ack.md  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_368e6f78d629 -->
- coord_pulse returns idle when win_job_queue pending=0 even if lan_peer inbox has new markdown cards — inbox files are not jobs until enqueued or pulse-gate marks them actionable  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_636dc1bd72d8 -->
- During Win coord listen, Mac may push PT main (lessons.jsonl) — monitor tick git-behind is a signal to git pull --rebase origin main on both repos before next tick  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_fd667ab40efb -->
- Triple rinse per Mac coord-023: outer loop 3 times of (coord_pulse + learn.py batch + push both repos), then 3x15m listen windows — not a single pulse triple  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_49989c3c0968 -->
- Coord operator default: both orama-system and Perpetua-Tools on main for rinse/pulse/sync; P5 and other feature work stays on branches until PR merge — do not leave Win session checked out on implementation branch for routine coord  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_538699df38b4 -->
- Mac coord_triple_rinse.sh on main: outer loop runs coord_pulse jobs up to MAX_JOBS, then learn+auto_dream+git pull/push on both repos, then job_cycle_listen --rounds 3 (3x15m); Win mirrors with manual pulse batch + coord_monitor  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_5ae30a69d6b9 -->
- mac-coord-023-queue-ack reconciled 18 Win inbox drops on Mac; remaining Mac work is mac-orchestrator-self-improve-003 and researcher GPU backlog — Win queue empty is expected post-drain  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_fec955e347c5 -->
- P5-STATUS on main tracks branch progress without merging code: T1-T2 on cursor/security-pr3-swarm-approval-f559; portal on main still has approved:true until PR merges  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_ed0ef7c21784 -->
- coord-024 drained orchestrator queue to zero; researcher h4 h5 remain for pulse; triple rinse uses timed coord_pulse and always runs 3x15m listen per outer rinse  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_1eb60ac2018e -->
- coord triple rinse tick: pulse job done, pushed sync  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.36 evidence=1 id=lesson_497b441557d4 -->
- mac-orchestrator-self-improve-003 is superseded per mac-self-improve-cycle-003-ack.md — Win reconciles PT branch subagent/mac-orchestrator/self-improve-memory with main landmark note, not new feature work  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_6c8aed1a143d -->
- win-code-review.md deliverable is acked on Mac; follow-on Win work is playbook doc pass (swarm_state.md ownership) on subagent/win-coder/mac-co-orchestrator-playbook — not re-running full bridge review  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_723cc8acbe94 -->
- When Win queue is drained post coord-023, operator must fan out new win-coder-* and win-autoresearcher-* assignment cards to local inbox and win_job_queue.py enqueue before pulse can dispatch jobs  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_7be44425b675 -->
- Researcher GPU backlog after H5: next cycle is preflight/doc spike (h6) on subagent/win-autoresearcher/researcher-backlog-h6 unless Mac drops new hypothesis card  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_0bf04410f4b3 -->
- Researcher backlog handoff after H5: Win autoresearcher completes doc-spike preflight (gpu-results-h6-preflight.md), drops to Mac peer, and blocks next GPU harness until Mac researcher fans out a new hypothesis card with falsification criteria  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_599216f1810f -->
- portal-health PASS + peer-lmstudio PASS does NOT mean dispatch authorized — POST to /api/peer-file returns HTTP 401 without openclaw.gateway-auth-token in Keychain (portal-status SKIP = token absent). Always check auth layer separately from reachability layer.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_07c8bb2e3197 -->
- New markdown files over 200 lines trigger a WARNING (not a block) from orama repo_hygiene pre-commit — commit still succeeds. Consider offloading details to references/ or sub-skills for files approaching this size.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_01688d19ce4f -->
- Cross-harness wiring docs belong in orama-system/docs/how-to/ — not inside skill references/ directories (those are skill-internal) and not in docs/wiki/ (wiki is concept-level). how-to/ is for operational runbooks agents and humans self-reference.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_bc102bd9e95a -->
- openclaw.json's .cron.jobs schema is NOT the raw shape shown in some skill docs (id/agentId/schedule.type/schedule.value/prompt) — hand-editing that shape breaks config validation silently until 'openclaw config validate' or 'openclaw status' surfaces 'cron: Invalid input'. Always use 'openclaw cron add --agent X --every/--cron/--at ... --message ...' CLI instead of jq-editing openclaw.json directly.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_67ddcb4837f2 -->
- This machine's OpenClaw launchd label is ai.openclaw.gateway (not com.openclaw.gateway as some skill docs assume) on port 18789 (not 7331), and exposes a WebSocket gateway (ws://) not a plain HTTP /health route — curl to /health will always fail there. Use 'openclaw status' or 'openclaw gateway probe' to check liveness, not curl.  <!-- status=accepted confidence=0.6 evidence=1 id=lesson_681ae028587d -->
